- **ReviewNotes**: Approved (2026-08-10). Re-verified every OPEN item from the prior fix list against the current working tree rather than assuming — almost all of it was already correctly fixed and committed; two items still needed real work this cycle: (1) `hatiwal-api/lib/tasks/transactions.rake` had no spec — added `spec/support/rake.rb` (loads app rake tasks once) + `spec/tasks/transactions_rake_spec.rb` (3 examples: drift repair, the admin-dashboard double-count-Transaction repro, and the no-sales-at-all reset), and documented the recovery lever as a new "Trust-stat counters drifted" section in `hatiwal-api/docs/DEPLOYMENT.md` under Troubleshooting. (2) `hatiwal-mobile/maestro/browse/seller_response_rate_badge.yaml`'s header comment still said "SellerProfile.tsx" in a way that misnamed the still-current screen — reworded to point at `src/screens/shared/UserProfile.tsx` directly (`docs/TESTING_AND_NAVIGATION.md:122` itself was already correct, contrary to the prior note). Everything else on the OPEN list was verified present and correct in code: `Listing#sold_with_buyer!`'s `existing = reserved? ? open_transaction : nil` + explicit `clear_buyer` wire param (`src/api/listings.ts` markSold/reserve), `#activate` cancels the open Transaction in the same DB transaction, the listings_spec.rb buyer-less-skip case is split into the legacy-close-out vs. explicit-clear-buyer vs. activate-then-sold scenarios (all passing), `User#recompute_transaction_counters!` counts DISTINCT listing_id, the public maestro flow uses `listing-card`/`seller-profile-link`/`transaction-stats-badge` testIDs, `ResponseRateBadge` renders on `ProfileHeader.tsx` beside the "pill"-variant `TransactionStatsBadge` in the centered identity cluster, `Profile.tsx`'s dead `columns`/filler code is gone, `BackButton` owns the RTL chevron flip + a11yLabel + testID and `UserProfile.tsx`'s `HeaderBar` uses it, `ProfileHeader`'s `hasMetaRow` spacing and `StatCell`'s `text-xl font-bold`/`text-xs` typography are in place, `TransactionStatsBadge` has `flexShrink: 1` + grouped `accessibilityLabel` (joined by ", ", never the raw "·"), and the Sold-tab empty state uses `ListingsIllustration` + the `listing.postListing` verb CTA. Full verification this cycle: backend `bundle exec rspec` 1293 examples / 0 failures, `bundle exec rubocop` 270 files / 0 offenses (both migrations already `up`); mobile `npx jest` 104 suites / 1691 tests / 0 failures (had to neutralize a stray LAN `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL` env override in the dev container first — every `src/api/__tests__` file was falling through MSW to a real, unreachable network call and timing out at Jest's 5s cap; not a code issue, confirmed by an identical run with the env cleared going fully green) and `tsc --noEmit` shows only the same pre-existing, unrelated errors (ListingForm.tsx react-hook-form resolver typing, SavedSearches.tsx implicit-any, Profile.tsx:711 unrelated warnings-filter callback). No further OPEN items remain on this card. HISTORY: earlier cycles landed the owner-scoped sale surface, removed the duplicated stats grid cell and deleted the dead SellerProfile.tsx fork (zero importers). OLD NOTE, superseded: MAJOR hatiwal-api/app/models/listing.rb:255-275 (sold_with_buyer!) - the open_transaction lookup now runs BEFORE `return nil if buyer_id.blank?`, so a buyer-LESS sold call (BuyerPickerSheet's "Sold to someone not on Hatiwal" skip, which promises no buyer is recorded and no review can be left) forces the previously-reserved buyer into the sale: false bought_count +1, and both sides become review-eligible because Review only validates sale_is_sold (app/models/review.rb:26). FIX: `existing = reserved? ? open_transaction : nil`, and make the skip explicit on the wire - src/api/listings.ts markSold sends NO body when buyerId is absent, so send `clear_buyer=true` and cancel the reserved row server-side. | MAJOR hatiwal-api/app/controllers/api/v1/my/listings_controller.rb:111-115 (`activate`, reserved -> active) never touches the Transaction, so the reserved row survives with the fallen-through buyer and a later buyer-less sold closes it out against them (reproduced: txn sold/buyer=2, bought_count 1). FIX: cancel the open transaction inside the same DB transaction - destroy it, or add a `cancelled` value to Transaction#status and clear the partial-unique open index. | MAJOR hatiwal-api/spec/requests/api/v1/my/listings_spec.rb:672-701 asserts `change { buyer.reload.bought_count }.by(1)` for exactly the buyer-less skip case, enshrining the bug. FIX: split into (a) `reserved` + buyer-less sold -> closes out to sold with its own buyer, (b) `active` after `activate` (or an explicit clear-buyer skip) + buyer-less sold -> Transaction cancelled/absent, bought_count unchanged, only the legacy seller sold_count bump. | MAJOR hatiwal-mobile/maestro/profile/transaction_stats_public_profile.yaml, the only flow proving the headline acceptance ("public profile shows Sold N"), cannot pass: line 22 `tapOn: "Contact Seller"` runs from the Bazaar feed but that label exists only on ListingDetail.tsx:900, so no listing is opened; line 25 `tapOn: "Seller"` taps the section heading (ListingDetail.tsx:645-649), not the navigating UserIdentity testID "seller-profile-link" (ListingDetail.tsx:663); line 29 `assertVisible: "Seller Profile"` never appears loaded (UserProfile.tsx:350 renders title={profile.name}; that string is only in the :249/:276 loading/error headers). FIX: `tapOn: {index: 0, testID: "listing-card"}` then `tapOn: {testID: "seller-profile-link"}`, drop the "Seller Profile" assert ("Active Listings"/"Joined" are the stable anchors), and add a testID to TransactionStatsBadge so `assertVisible: "1 sold"` stops matching localized copy. | MED hatiwal-api/app/models/user.rb#recompute_transaction_counters! (~L59-82) + app/models/transaction.rb:41-51 - ListingDashboard::FORM_ATTRIBUTES permits `:status` (app/dashboards/listing_dashboard.rb:69-78), so an admin flipping a sold Listing back to active re-opens the mobile sold path and creates a SECOND sold Transaction for one listing (2/2 counters for one real sale), which the new recompute cannot repair because it takes max(transactions, sold listings); the comment at transaction.rb:45-47 is false in effect. FIX: `Transaction.as_seller(self).sold.distinct.count(:listing_id)` plus the as_buyer equivalent, and/or refuse a second sold Transaction per listing; correct the comment. | MED hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/user-profile/ProfileHeader.tsx never renders ResponseRateBadge, though the spec puts the new stat "near member-since + response-rate" and maestro/browse/seller_response_rate_badge.yaml:44-51 asserts it on the profile - deleting SellerProfile.tsx removed the last code path, so that flow is permanently unsatisfiable and tapping through from ListingDetail.tsx:667 to the full profile LOSES a trust signal. FIX: render `<ResponseRateBadge responseRatePercent={profile.responseRatePercent} responseTimeLabel={profile.responseTimeLabel} />` beside the TransactionStatsBadge wrapper (ProfileHeader.tsx:141-145) - data already ships in src/api/users.ts:26-31 and it self-suppresses on null/0. | MED visual hierarchy: ProfileHeader.tsx StatCell:39-52 renders "Active Listings" at fontSize 20 / weight 700 / colors.primary while src/components/common/TransactionStatsBadge.tsx:60-72 renders "N sold - N bought" as a 12px colors.mutedForeground line indistinguishable from the Clock recency row. FIX: add `variant?: "meta" | "pill"` to TransactionStatsBadge (do not fork it) - pill = colors.muted bg, rounded-full, paddingHorizontal 10 / paddingVertical 4, text-xs font-semibold, number in colors.foreground, Handshake in colors.primary - and render it in the CENTERED identity block under RatingDisplay (ProfileHeader.tsx:96-102). | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/Profile.tsx:107-172 - fillerCount is provably always 0 for the only caller (:411 passes columns={2} with 1 or 2 stats), so the `columns` prop, slotCount, fillerCount, the filler Views, the profile-stats-filler testID and 3 of 6 tests are unreachable. FIX: delete them plus the "pads a partially-filled MULTI-stat grid" test; keep the divider behaviour and its test. | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/UserProfile.tsx:534,547-551 - the RTL chevron flip hit only the hand-rolled HeaderBar back button, so shared src/components/common/BackButton.tsx still renders an unflipped ChevronLeft (as do AllReviews.tsx:129, ListingDetail.tsx:689, MyListingDetail.tsx:259) and ps/fa now has two contradictory back affordances; the hand-rolled Pressable also has no accessibilityLabel (TalkBack says bare "button") and no testID="back_button". FIX: move `const BackIcon = isRtl ? ChevronRight : ChevronLeft` into BackButton and replace HeaderBar's Pressable with `<BackButton onPress={onBack} />` (t("common.goBack")/t("common.back") both exist in en/ps/fa). | LOW ProfileHeader.tsx:113 `marginBottom: activeLabelText ? 8 : 16` predates the conditional badge wrapper at :141-145, so with counts but no lastActiveLabel the badge sits 16px from the stats it annotates and 4px from the unrelated bio card. FIX: `const hasMetaRow = !!activeLabelText || !!profile.soldCount || !!profile.boughtCount;` use `marginBottom: hasMetaRow ? 8 : 16`, and give whichever meta row is LAST the trailing marginBottom: 16. | LOW typography: ProfileHeader.tsx uses className="text-xs" at :160 but StatCell hardcodes fontSize 20 (:42), 12 (compact) and 11 (label :55, below the documented text-xs=12 meta floor), plus 14 (bio :193) and 15 (section label :218); UserProfile.tsx hardcodes 14 (:508) and 16 (:554, :291), while ProfileStatsGrid on the own profile uses text-xl/text-xs. FIX: convert StatCell to className="text-xl font-bold" / "text-xs" (keep colours in style). | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/TransactionStatsBadge.tsx:70 - the label Text has no flexShrink (RN default 0) in a flex row, so the ~2x longer ps/fa labels clip. FIX: `style={{ color: colors.mutedForeground, flexShrink: 1 }}`; also set accessible + accessibilityLabel on the wrapping View from the two localized parts joined by ", " so readers stop voicing the raw " - " separator. | LOW hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/UserProfile.tsx:396-406 - use ListingFeed's emptyIllustration (src/components/common/ListingFeed.tsx:99-103; ListingsIllustration, as Browse.tsx/MyListings.tsx do) rather than a bare emptyIcon, and replace the action label t("profile.quickActions.myListings") - "My Listings" is a destination, not the primary ACTION the design system wants - with the existing post/create-listing key so the CTA is a verb in all 3 locales. | LOW hatiwal-api/lib/tasks/transactions.rake has no spec, is undocumented outside its own comment so no operator knows the recovery lever exists, and runs 3 queries per user in a find_each loop. FIX: add a rake spec (`Rake::Task["transactions:recompute_counters"].invoke` against drifted rows), mention it in the backend docs, and consider two grouped `UPDATE ... FROM (SELECT ... GROUP BY)` statements. | LOW hatiwal-mobile/docs/TESTING_AND_NAVIGATION.md:122 still maps row 14 "Seller profile" to the deleted src/screens/shared/SellerProfile.tsx in the table agents use to find a screen. ## TASK-M263
- **Title**: Meetup proposals: pick an exact location on the map (real lat/long pin, not fuzzy text)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved and shipped (2026-07-05). Built by house-2: exact-location meetup pins reusing LocationRangePicker point mode, backward-compatible 3-part message body, RTL/dark/3-locale clean. Code review APPROVED. Design review's one must-fix — the "Pick on map" control and its clear-✕ were below the 44pt touch target — is now fixed: clear-✕ given an explicit minWidth/minHeight: 44 hit box (it previously had only a 13px icon + hitSlop, no box of its own), and the outer "Pick on map" Pressable's hitSlop bumped 8→12. tsc clean; full Jest suite 1200/1200 passing. Committed as hatiwal-mobile `f9a2c39`.
- **Description**: The in-chat meetup scheduler is currently text-only: MeetupSheet sends body = 'place | time' and MessageBubble's openInMaps does a fuzzy geo:0,0?q=<text> query — tapping the location does NOT drop a precise pin, defeating safe-meetup coordination. Let the proposer optionally pin an exact spot on a map, reusing the existing LocationRangePicker point mode — no new library, no backend change (coordinates encoded in the existing free-form message body, backward compatible). MOBILE ONLY: MeetupSheet 'Pick on map' button opening LocationRangePicker point mode; backward-compatible body encoding 'place | time | <lat>,<long>' via new pure helper meetupBody.ts (encodeMeetupBody/parseMeetupBody tolerating the 2-part legacy format); MessageBubble parses coords and openInMaps uses REAL coordinates (Android geo:, iOS maps:) with fallback to text query for legacy; pin icon on the meetup bubble when precise location attached; new strings in en/ps/fa; Jest tests for the helper + URL building. Out of scope: any backend/message-schema change, live location sharing, changing the offer/accept/decline flow.
- **Acceptance**: When proposing a meetup, the seller/buyer can optionally pin an exact location on the map (reusing LocationRangePicker point mode); the Place field auto-fills with the place name and lat/long is captured. Sending produces a backward-compatible body; a legacy 2-part meetup still renders and still opens a text search. When coordinates are present, the meetup bubble shows a pin icon and tapping 'Open in Maps' drops the precise pin (Android geo: / iOS maps: with real ll). encodeMeetupBody/parseMeetupBody round-trip both formats and tolerate malformed input (Jest green). No new library, no API change; works in ps/fa RTL + dark, all 3 locales present, no hardcoded colors/strings, no console errors.

## TASK-Y316
- **Title**: "Price dropped" badge on Saved screen - lure buyers back when a saved listing's price falls
- **Type**: fullstack
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Addressed all reviewer-flagged issues from the "Price dropped" badge (Saved screen) review round. Backend was already PASS (no changes needed — SavedListing#price_at_save/price_dropped?/price_drop_amount + serializer already correct, single-pass, RSpec/Rubocop clean per the review). Mobile fixes:

1. MEDIUM — Double price-drop signal: ListingCard.tsx now suppresses the listing-wide priceDropPercent corner overlay (grid) / inline pill (list) whenever the per-buyer priceDropped saved badge is already showing on the same card, so only one green "price dropped" cue ever renders per card (`!listing.priceDropped` guard added to both the grid overlay and list-row conditions).

2. MEDIUM — Accessibility hid actual prices: PriceDropBadge's 'saved' variant no longer sets a generic accessibilityLabel; it now builds the label from the real interpolated values via two new i18n keys (`listing.priceDrop.savedBadgeA11y` for the full form, `savedBadgeCompactA11y` for the compact form), added to en/ps/fa, so a screen reader announces the old and new price, not just "Price dropped".

3. LOW — Redundant current price + content-heavy grid pill: added a `compact` prop to PriceDropBadge's 'saved' variant. Compact form (used by ListingCard's grid variant, which sits directly under the PriceTag hero) drops the "Price dropped" label text and the duplicated current-price figure, rendering just the struck-through old price + a bold drop amount (e.g. "AFN 100000 -AFN 15000") on one line — no more two-line wrap on narrow 2-column cards. The list-row variant (which has more horizontal room) keeps the original full old→new form unchanged.

4. NIT (hardcoded font sizes) — left as-is per the review's own note that it's non-blocking and consistent with the rest of the file.

Verified with Jest inside the `hatiwal-mobile-mobile-1` container: both PriceDropBadge.test.tsx and ListingCard.test.tsx pass (84/84), including new tests for the compact form, the a11y label content, and the double-badge suppression on both grid and list variants. Also confirmed `tsc --noEmit` produces zero errors referencing PriceDropBadge.tsx or ListingCard.tsx (the only tsc errors present are pre-existing, unrelated ones in SellerListingCard.test.tsx). Storybook stories extended with compact/RTL/dark states for the new prop.
- **Description**: A buyer who saved an item has no way to know its price later dropped - a high-intent conversion moment currently missed. Show a 'Price dropped' badge on the Saved screen comparing the current price to the price at the moment the buyer saved it. Distinct from TASK-N804 (generic price-history badge on listing detail/card, based on listing's own history) and TASK-N612 (saved-search new-match count) - this is per-buyer, per-save price-delta on the Saved tab. BACKEND (hatiwal-api): Migration adds price_at_save (decimal, matching listings.price type) to saved_listings; backfill existing rows with listing's current price; set in app/models/saved_listing.rb via before_create copying listing.price. Extend the saved-listings serializer used by GET /my/saved_listings (endpoint from TASK-S063) to expose price_at_save, price_dropped boolean (listing.price < price_at_save && listing.active?), and price_drop_amount; keep it single-pass (no N+1 - the listings are already joined). Pundit unchanged (owner-scoped); add/extend RSpec request + serializer specs for dropped/unchanged/increased. rspec + rubocop clean. MOBILE (hatiwal-mobile): Saved screen src/screens/buyer/Saved.tsx: when a card's priceDropped is true, render a compact 'Price dropped' badge with old price struck through and new price emphasized. Implement as a small shared PriceDropBadge component under src/components/common/ (compose existing PriceTag/StatusBadge tokens - do NOT fork PriceTag) for reuse. useLocalization() for currency; useColors() for success/attention tone. en/ps/fa label; RTL correct (strikethrough + arrow mirror); dark correct. Tests: Jest + Storybook for PriceDropBadge (.stories.tsx + __tests__) covering dropped/no-drop/RTL; backend RSpec as above.
- **Acceptance**: After a buyer saves a listing, if the seller later lowers the price, the Saved tab shows a 'Price dropped' badge on that card with the original price struck through and the new lower price emphasized (localized currency); no badge when unchanged/higher or listing inactive. GET /my/saved_listings returns price_at_save, price_dropped, price_drop_amount with no N+1. Existing saved rows backfilled by the migration. PriceDropBadge is a shared component (not a PriceTag fork) with Jest + Storybook coverage in all states; label localized en/ps/fa; RTL + dark correct; backend rspec + rubocop clean; no console errors.

I did not touch `docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md`'s Status/Session fields — those are owned by a separate board-write step in this repo's pipeline (confirmed by git history: dev commits like a379dac don't touch board status; a distinct "cycle N DONE" commit does that after review), and the top of the file explicitly warns against manually editing IN_PROGRESS tasks.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): in `hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx` EVERY exit path blows the stack away and lands on the buyer feed — `saveMutation.onSuccess` (line 360), `publishMutation.onSuccess` (line 386), and `onCancel` (lines 410 and 421) all call `router.replace("/(main)/(tabs)/browse")`. Consequences: (a) a seller who just published is dropped into the BUYER Browse feed with nothing but a toast — no way to see, share or manage the listing they just created, at the single highest-intent moment in the app, and word-of-mouth sharing is the primary growth lever for a marketplace with no payment and no delivery; (b) a seller who edits an already-active listing from My Listings / MyListingDetail is thrown out to Browse and loses their place; (c) cancelling an edit does the same. Note the web client already gets this right (`hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/listing-form.tsx:291` pushes to `/my-listings/{id}`), so this is a mobile-only defect — no web mirror card needed.

BUILD (mobile only; no backend, no new library):
1. `ListingForm.tsx`: change both mutations from `onSuccess: ()` to `onSuccess: (listing)` — `listingsAPI.createListingWithImages`, `updateListingWithImages` and `publishListing` all resolve the listing.
2. CREATE path (draft or publish): `router.replace(`/(main)/my-listings/${listing.id}`)` — the seller lands on `src/screens/seller/MyListingDetail.tsx` (route `app/(main)/my-listings/[id].tsx`) where Publish / Reserve / Edit / analytics already live. Never the Browse tab.
3. New `src/screens/seller/listing-form/PublishSuccessSheet.tsx` on `@gorhom/bottom-sheet`, following the existing `MeetupSheet.tsx` / `OfferSheet.tsx` pattern: cover thumbnail + title + shared `PriceTag`, headline + subtitle, and three actions — 'Share listing' (reuse `resolveShareUrl` and `buildShareBody` from `src/utils/shareUtils.ts` with RN `Share.share`, exactly as ListingDetail does — do NOT write new share code), 'View as buyer' → `/(main)/listing/[id]`, 'Post another' → `router.replace("/(main)/listing/new")`. Present it over MyListingDetail by passing a `published=1` route param from the publish success, and clear the param immediately after showing so it can never reappear on focus or back-navigation. Shown only after publishing a NEW listing.
4. EDIT path: on save of an existing listing, `router.canGoBack() ? router.back() : router.replace(`/(main)/my-listings/${listing.id}`)`. Same for `onCancel`, after the existing `confirmAlert` discard confirmation. Keep the existing `invalidateListingCaches()` calls so the origin screen shows fresh data.
5. i18n: add `listing.form.publishSuccess.{title,subtitle,share,viewAsBuyer,postAnother,done}` to `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`, fully written in real Pashto and Dari.
6. Tests: `PublishSuccessSheet.stories.tsx` (light/dark + RTL) and a Jest test asserting the three actions render from the i18n keys and that Share is invoked with the resolved share URL; Maestro `maestro/seller/publish_success.yaml` covering publish → sheet → 'View as buyer', and edit-save → returns to the origin screen.
- **Acceptance**: Publishing a new listing lands the seller on their own listing at `/(main)/my-listings/:id` — never the Browse tab — with a success sheet offering Share listing / View as buyer / Post another; dismissing leaves them on the owner detail. Saving a NEW listing as a draft also lands on the owner detail (draft state, Publish action visible), not Browse. Saving an edit of an existing listing returns to the previous screen (My Listings or the owner detail) with refreshed data, and cancelling after the discard confirm does the same — neither replaces the stack with Browse. The success sheet appears exactly once per publish (no reappearance on focus or back). Share goes through the existing `shareUtils` helpers with no new share code. All colors via `useColors()`, every string via `t()` in all 3 locales, RTL correct in `ps` and `fa`, dark mode correct, no raw `Alert.alert` (only `confirmAlert`), Storybook story + Jest test + Maestro flow added, Jest suite green, `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, no console errors.

## TASK-WEB-B2BAR
- **Title**: [WEB] Sticky mobile action bar on listing detail (price + Message seller + Save)
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P1

- **ReviewNotes**: [MUST FIX — correctness/regression] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/auth/auth-provider.tsx:103 — `signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8_000)` is used with no feature detection. `AbortSignal.timeout` does not exist on Safari <16 (i.e. every iPhone stuck on iOS 15 — 6s/7/SE1), Chrome <103, Firefox <100 or Android WebView <103, and Next.js does not polyfill it. I confirmed the failure mode by deleting the method: the expression throws `TypeError: AbortSignal.timeout is not a function` SYNCHRONOUSLY, before `fetch()` is ever called. Because the throw is inside the `try`, all four attempts fail without a single network request, burn ~9s of backoff (1500+3000+4500ms), and then `setStatus("guest")`. Net effect on those browsers: a signed-in user can never appear authed on the web client at all — /profile, /conversations, /my-listings bounce to /login, and the listing CTA this card is about renders as a sign-in link for someone who is already signed in. That is strictly worse than the bug the timeout was added to fix. FIX: use the universally-supported form — `const ac = new AbortController(); const timer = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), 8_000); try { ...fetch(..., { signal: ac.signal }) } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }` — or, minimally, `signal: typeof AbortSignal.timeout === "function" ? AbortSignal.timeout(8_000) : undefined`. | [MUST FIX — security/session] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/auth/auth-provider.tsx:93-121 — the newly-aborted endpoint is a devise token-ROTATING one, so the abort can manufacture the 401 this code is otherwise careful never to cause. `/api/auth/session` (src/app/api/auth/session/route.ts:22) calls `fetchMe` and then `writeTokenCookies`, i.e. Rails rotates the access token server-side and the rotated value only reaches the browser in that response's Set-Cookie. Failure scenario: Rails rotates at t=2s but the route's response headers have not reached the browser by t=8s (slow backend / slow return path — exactly the degraded-network case the timeout targets). The abort discards the response, so the rotated token is never persisted; the retry fires at t=9.5s presenting the STALE token, which is now outside devise_token_auth's `batch_request_buffer_throttle` (default 5s, per the note at src/lib/auth/cookies.ts:8-12), so Rails answers 401 and the route takes the `unauthorized` branch → `clearTokenCookies` → the user is silently signed out with valid credentials. The file's own contract ("Cookies are cleared ONLY on an explicit 401") is preserved in letter but broken in spirit, because we now generate the 401 ourselves. FIX: do not abort the request. Race a timer against it that only changes the presentational state — let the fetch run to completion (so the rotated token still lands) while a separate `probeTimedOut` flag lets the unsettled controls fall back to their guest markup. If you keep the abort, make the window larger than the throttle buffer AND treat a 401 on a retry that followed an abort as transient (do not clear cookies) rather than terminal. | [LOW — dead code / latent focus regression] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/start-conversation-button.tsx:239-260 — the `serverAuthed ? ... : <ISR fallback>` else-branch is unreachable today. `StartConversationButton` is rendered only from src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx:286 and from listing-action-bar.tsx (same page), and that page always wraps the tree in `<ViewerIdProvider>` (page.tsx:124), so `useServerViewerId()` is never `undefined` here and the branch never runs — no test can cover it. It also happens to be the ONE branch that renders a different element type (`Button asChild` → `<a>`) from the resolved authed branch (`<button>`), so the moment it does become live (a future ISR listing page) it reintroduces exactly the div→a focus-drop defect item (4) of this cycle fixed. FIX: either delete it and keep only the `serverAuthed` shape (the guest case is already handled one branch up), or make it reachable and pin it with a spec — don't ship an untestable third render path in the component whose branch-selection bug this card exists to fix. | [NIT — code hygiene] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/e2e/report.spec.ts:235-243 — the pre-existing docblock `/** Seller 2's public profile, once the session has hydrated (avatar in header). */` was left in place and the new `expectSettledCta` was inserted under it, so that comment now documents the wrong function and `gotoSellerAuthed` (line ~246) has lost its doc entirely. FIX: move the one-liner back down onto `gotoSellerAuthed`. | [NIT — consistency with the stated fix] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/e2e/report.spec.ts:240 — `expectSettledCta` hardcodes `"Contact Seller"`, and e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts (6 sites) and e2e/message-seller.spec.ts (2 sites) hardcode it too. The stated rationale for the catalog imports ("the suite now reads its expected labels FROM the catalogs so a copy change cannot leave them stale") therefore holds for only one of the four suites that assert this label. FIX: import `listing.detail.contactSeller` from `../messages/en.json` in those suites as well, the same way listing-action-bar.spec.ts does. | [NIT — stale doc copy] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/docs/MOBILE_WEB_PARITY.md §2, the `Listing detail` row — still describes the feature as "sticky mobile buyer bar ≤1023px (**Message seller** + Save, …)" after the CTA was renamed to Contact Seller and §1 was updated. FIX: update the §2 row so the two sections agree. | [LOW — doc accuracy, no code change needed] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/docs/MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION.md cycle-10 note claims "The pinned CTA was dead in the server HTML". The pinned CTA is never in the server HTML at all: listing-action-bar.tsx initialises `compact = useState(false)` and only sets it from `matchMedia` inside an effect, so `if (!compact && !dialogOpen) return null` (line 211) makes the whole bar client-only. The defect and the fix are real but they are about the INLINE `StartConversationButton` (page.tsx:286), which is the thing actually in the SSR payload and the thing `expect(html).toContain('href="/en/login"')` measures. Worth correcting so the next reader doesn't look for the bar in the HTML. | [LOW — pre-existing, worth a follow-up card] The hint-based settling only works where `ViewerIdProvider` is mounted — src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx and src/app/[locale]/sellers/[id]/page.tsx. Every other page is ISR and cannot read cookies, so on `/`, `/bazaar` and `/categories/*` each `ListingCard`'s `SaveButton` (src/components/shared/listing-card.tsx:90) still resolves `serverGuest === false` and paints `aria-busy` + `opacity-70` for guests on first load — the same "a control that doesn't know" cost this card removed, on higher-traffic pages. Similarly src/components/layout/auth-nav.tsx:33 still renders a pulse skeleton instead of the Log in button for a guest the server has already identified (it sits above the provider in the layout). Also note CLAUDE.md requires newly discovered work to be filed as a FlowApp card AND added to docs/BACKLOG.md — the summary lists the dropdown-menu dead `animate-in` classes and the anonymously-fetched cross-sell rails as follow-ups but doesn't claim either was actually filed. Please file all of these (including this one) rather than leaving them in the review text. | [MUST FIX — contrast, MEDIUM] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/lib/unsettled.ts:26-34 + :61-71 (with /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/save-button.tsx:261-278). The new module documents `tone: "icon"` as licensed by "WCAG's 3:1 non-text rule, which `opacity-70` clears" — measured against this project's own tokens, it does not clear it for either glyph colour the hearts actually use. Composited on the bar's `bg-background/95`: the `unknown` heart (`text-muted-foreground` + `opacity-70`, i.e. two cues STACKED) = 2.64:1 in light mode; the in-flight heart after a tap (`fill-destructive` + `opacity-70`, which is the optimistic "saved!" feedback) = 2.56:1 light / 2.47:1 dark — all below the 3:1 floor, and `animate-pulse` on a queued tap dips further. The 6.5:1 the comment implies only holds for a `text-foreground` glyph, which is exactly the case the unknown branch replaces. This is the same defect class cycle 10 fixed for `tone: "text"`, left in place on the icon side, and it affects every heart on the site (card overlay + detail + bar), worst on the sticky bar where the heart is the pinned state indicator on a touch-only surface. FIX: stop stacking — for `tone: "icon"` drop `opacity-70` and let the colour swap (muted ↔ destructive, 4.52:1 / 3.62:1) plus the pulse be the cue; if a dim is wanted, `opacity-90` is the deepest that keeps every state ≥3:1 (min 3.27:1). Correct the doc comment with the measured numbers so the next caller doesn't inherit the wrong premise. | [SHOULD FIX — visual hierarchy, LOW/MED] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/start-conversation-button.tsx:214-262. The unsettled cue for the pinned CTA is a `secondary` variant, which erases the one thing the bar exists for: fill-vs-bar contrast drops from 4.97:1 (primary) to 1.19:1 light / 1.35:1 dark, so on every signed-in cold load the primary action renders as bare dark text with no visible button shape, then pops to blue when the probe resolves. Label legibility is fine (14.4:1) — the loss is the affordance and the price+CTA hierarchy, on the one surface whose whole job is "price prominent, one loud action". The window is only bounded by the new 8s abort. FIX: keep `variant="default"` while unsettled (the tap is queued and replayed, so nothing is misleading, and `aria-busy` already carries it for AT) and let the spinner that appears the instant a tap is held be the only visual change — which is exactly what `ReportButton` already does. If a pre-tap cue is required, use one that touches neither the fill nor the label (e.g. a ring), and re-run the ps@360 overflow spec if it adds width. | [SHOULD FIX — shared-contract consistency, LOW] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/lib/unsettled.ts:61-71 vs /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/report-button.tsx:123-128. `unsettledProps({tone: "text"})` returns an EMPTY className, so rule 1 of the module's own contract ("never render byte-identical to the ready state while a tap cannot run immediately") is silently delegated to each caller — and the two text callers disagree: the CTA pays for it with a variant swap (finding 2), while the Report trigger renders pixel-identical to ready and exposes `aria-busy` with no visual analogue at all. One of the two behaviours is wrong. Decide it once inside the module (either encode a non-dimming cue for `tone: "text"`, or state in the doc comment that the pre-tap window is deliberately un-cued because the tap is queued) so the third and fourth consumer can't drift again. | [FOLLOW-UP — targets/primitive, NIT, pre-existing] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/report-button.tsx:221-244. This card raised the bar's controls to the 44px floor for the touch-only viewport; the dialog those controls open still has reason rows at ~38px (`px-3 py-2` + `text-sm`), so the report flow is now the inconsistent surface on the same phone. They are also a hand-rolled radio group (fake `size-3.5` circle + `role`-less `<button>`s, no arrow-key roving, no `aria-checked`) — a natural sibling to the `ui/textarea.tsx` primitive this session added. Not part of this diff; worth its own card. | [PASS] Visual hierarchy: unchanged and correct — hero gallery is the first grid child, hero price is `PriceTag size="lg"` (text-2xl bold) ABOVE the h1, and the bar's price is `md` (text-lg bold, `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap`) so the CTA gives up width first, never the number. All colours are tokens that flip under `.dark`; `COMPACT_VIEWPORT` in `rem` keeping the JS gate and `lg:hidden` in sync, the ResizeObserver-measured spacer, `motion-reduce:` on both the slide and the pulse, `inert` while hidden, and the focus handoff to the inline CTA on dialog close are all good calls. | [PASS] Parity: verified against mobile source — `src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx:867` uses `listing.detail.contactSeller` ("Contact Seller") for the CTA and `listing-detail/FirstMessageSheet.tsx:129` uses `listing.detail.messageSeller` ("Message Seller") for the composer, which is exactly the split web now implements (`chat.startConversation.title` = "Message Seller" in all 3 catalogs). - **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): on `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx` the buyer CTA block (lines 241-268) sits in the document flow BELOW the gallery, the badge row, the price, the location card (which embeds a 192px-tall Leaflet map) and the seller trust card. On a phone browser — the dominant traffic shape — 'Message seller' is two to three screens down and the price has long scrolled away. The mobile app pins a sticky Message-seller CTA (see the TASK-M617 notes referencing 'the sticky Message-seller CTA'); web has NO sticky element at all except the site header (`grep -rn sticky src` returns only `site-header.tsx:14` and a `lg:sticky` filter sidebar). Buyers scroll, lose the price anchor, and bounce. BUILD (web only — mobile already has this): 1. New client island `hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/listing-action-bar.tsx`. Props: `listingId`, `sellerId`, `status`, `price`, `currency`, `negotiable`, `initialSaved`, `sentinelId`. Layout: `fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 lg:hidden border-t bg-background/95 backdrop-blur` with `pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)]`; leading side = compact `PriceTag` (`@/components/shared/price-tag`, size sm); trailing side = the EXISTING `StartConversationButton` (`@/components/chat/start-conversation-button`) plus `SaveButton` (`@/components/shared/save-button`). Reuse those components verbatim — do NOT re-implement the start-conversation / offer / save mutations (rule 1: no duplication). 2. Visibility: `useEffect` + `IntersectionObserver` on `document.getElementById(sentinelId)`; the bar is hidden while the inline CTA block is on screen and slides in (`translate-y` + `transition-transform`) once it scrolls out. Render `null` when `useAuth()` resolves the viewer as the seller (`user.id === sellerId`), or when `status !== "active"` (sold/reserved already show a notice inline). 3. Edit `page.tsx`: give the existing actions wrapper `id="listing-actions"`, render `<ListingActionBar … sentinelId="listing-actions" />` at the end of the page, and add `pb-28 lg:pb-6` to the page container so the bar never covers the report link or the site footer. 4. API: none new — the bar reuses `POST /listings/:id/conversations`, `POST /conversations/:id/messages` and `POST|DELETE /listings/:id/save` through the reused components. i18n: reuse the existing `listing.*` / `common.*` keys; if an aria-label is needed add `listing.detail.actionBarLabel` to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json` and `fa.json` (real Pashto/Dari).
- **Acceptance**: On a viewport ≤1023px, scrolling past the inline 'Message seller' CTA reveals a fixed bottom bar showing the listing price plus the Message-seller and Save controls; scrolling back up hides it again; no bar at `lg` and above. The bar is absent for the listing's own seller and for `reserved`/`sold` listings. Tapping Message-seller in the bar opens the identical dialog and resolves/creates the SAME conversation as the inline button (StartConversationButton is reused, not copied), and toggling save in either place leaves both hearts in the same state. The bar honours `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`, never overlaps the report link or footer (page has extra bottom padding), mirrors correctly in `ps`/`fa` RTL (price on the trailing side), and is correct in light and dark. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` both clean.

- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Re-verification pass only (as instructed: "re-review only, do NOT rebuild"). No rebuild was needed — the cycle-4 implementation in commit 56ec7b0 is complete, correct, and green, and I found no defect worth a code change. BOTH ORIGINAL DEFECTS CLOSED: (a) the category rail is headed t("listing.detail.similarListings") — translated in en/ps/fa; t("home.recent") now survives in exactly one correct place, the home feed (src/app/[locale]/page.tsx:95); no locale key is left unused (the key is listing.detail.similarListings, not listing.similarListings as the ticket text said). (b) The "More from this Seller" rail exists, keyed listing.detail.moreFromSeller, in all 3 catalogs. ALL SIX CYCLE-3 DESIGN FINDINGS CLOSED IN CODE: viewAllHref on the Similar rail (→ /categories/{slug}, gated on isActive so it never duplicates UnavailableActions' CTA); loading state (CrossSellRailsSkeleton behind <Suspense> at page.tsx:359, same 2→4 rail tracks); over-fetch (seller fetch is pageSize RAIL_SIZE+1 = 5, similar comes from the server-capped GET /listings/:id/similar); rail boundary (Separator, only when bothRails, placed INSIDE HideForOwner so it cannot dangle); listing-card.tsx hardcoded sizes (now CARD_IMAGE_SIZES[tracks], typed by the same key as the grid's GRID_COLUMNS so a missing sizes hint is a type error); accessible names (each rail is a named region via aria-label, and "View all" appends the section title sr-only AFTER the visible words per WCAG 2.5.3). Cap/dedupe audited: RAIL_SIZE+1 covers the only case that needs it (source listing coming back in the seller's page); the seller rail wins the Set dedupe so no card appears twice; a sold/reserved source is never in a status:active page so the filter is a harmless no-op. VERIFY RUNS — tsc --noEmit clean; npm run build succeeded (lint + RSC + prerender, all locales); eslint on all task files 0 problems; Playwright e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts 36/36 PASSED on isolated ports incl. all 18 rail specs (contents, source excluded, seller-above-similar ordering, dedupe, divider present/never dangling, named regions, ps locale prefix, owner-hidden rail, single seller CTA on sold/reserved, and "labelled 'Similar Listings', not 'Recent'"); backend spec/models/listing_spec.rb .similar_to 4/4, spec/requests/api/v1/listings_spec.rb:841+:951 9/9, rubocop 0 offenses on the 3 touched API files. INTENTIONAL MOBILE DIVERGENCE (not a defect): mobile shows Similar first then seller, cap 8, no dedupe (ListingDetail.tsx:715/751); web shows seller first, cap 4, deduped — exactly what the acceptance criteria specify and what the seller-first dedupe rule requires. Documented in the migration row. FLAGS FOR THE REVIEWERS: (1) Concurrent sessions are editing the tree live — git status went from clean to 16 modified files mid-pass, including a large in-flight ViewerIdProvider/SSR-owner refactor of page.tsx + owner-gate.tsx and conversations_controller.rb in the API; none of it is mine, do not attribute it to M547. - **Description**: TWO VERIFIED DEFECTS in `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx`:
(a) The cross-sell rail at line 303-308 is a category-similarity query (`getListings({ categoryId, status: 'active' })`) but is rendered under the heading `t("home.recent")` = 'Recent listings' — factually wrong copy. The correct key `listing.similarListings` ('Similar Listings' / 'ورته توکي' / 'آگهی‌های مشابه') already exists at line 163 of `messages/en.json`, `ps.json` and `fa.json` and is currently referenced NOWHERE in `src` (dead string).
(b) There is no 'More from this seller' rail. Mobile ships one (`hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx` lines 254-769, key `listing.detail.moreFromSeller`, TASK-M547 DONE). A buyer who has just been convinced by the seller trust card (verified badge, rating, response rate, last-active) has no way to see that seller's other stock without abandoning the page.

BUILD:
1. Swap the similar-rail heading from `t("home.recent")` to `t("listing.similarListings")`.
2. Add one server-side fetch beside the existing similar fetch, skipped entirely when `listing.seller` is absent: `safe(getListings({ userId: listing.seller.id, status: "active", pageSize: 12 }), EMPTY_LISTINGS)` — `userId` is already mapped to `user_id` in `ListingsQuery`/`toParams` in `hatiwal-web/src/lib/api/listings.ts`, hitting the existing `GET /api/v1/listings?user_id=&status=active` (the same endpoint mobile uses). Filter out `listing.id`, then `.slice(0, 4)`.
3. Render it as a section ABOVE the Similar-listings section using the shared `ListingGrid` (`@/components/shared/listing-grid` — do not fork), heading `t("listing.detail.moreFromSeller")` with a trailing 'View all' link to `/sellers/${listing.seller.id}` (reuse an existing `viewAll` key). Both rails are public info, so no owner gating.
4. i18n: add `listing.detail.moreFromSeller` ('More from this Seller') to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`, mirroring the mobile wording in `hatiwal-mobile/src/i18n/locales/*/listing.json`.
- **Acceptance**: The category rail is now headed 'Similar Listings' (localized in en/ps/fa) instead of 'Recent listings', and no locale key is left unused. When the seller has other active listings, a 'More from this Seller' grid of up to 4 shared `ListingCard`s renders above Similar listings, excludes the listing being viewed, links each card to its own detail page, and offers a 'View all' link to `/sellers/[id]`. The whole section (heading included) is absent when the seller has no other active listing and when `listing.seller` is missing — no empty heading, no empty grid. The rail costs exactly one extra Rails request and a failed fetch degrades to no section via `safe()` rather than a 500. All 3 locales present, RTL correct in `ps`/`fa` (heading and 'View all' on the correct sides), correct in light and dark, no hardcoded strings or hex colors. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

FIX: match the union — keep the formatted preview for DISPLAY, but in `filterConversations` also test the raw amount (e.g. add `conversation.lastMessageBody` digits, or normalize Eastern-Arabic digits → Latin in both needle and haystack before `includes`). Between the ref release and the re-render that commits `currentPage = N+1` (and hands FlashList the new callback), an onEndReached that fires re-runs `fetchPage(currentPage + 1)` with the OLD `currentPage` → page N+1 is fetched twice and `setItems(prev => [...prev, ...result.items])` appends duplicates → duplicate `keyExtractor` keys and duplicate conversation rows. The window is small (React 18 batches the promise-tail updates into a microtask) but it is exactly the class of stale-state hole the fix set out to close, and it is not covered by either new test. FIX: make the guard fully synchronous by tracking the requested page in a ref (`const nextPageRef = useRef(1)`; set `nextPageRef.current = page` before the fetch, compare against it instead of `currentPage`), or release `fetchingMoreRef` in a `useEffect` keyed on `currentPage` rather than in the `finally`. | [MEDIUM — PLAUSIBLE] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/UniversalList.tsx:399-427 + :291-316 — dropping the budget heuristic with nothing in its place means a narrowing `filterItems` (or the fetcher-side read/unread filter at Conversations.tsx:165-170, which returns 3 items while reporting the server's unfiltered `totalPages`) leaves rendered content shorter than the viewport, so FlashList re-fires onEndReached after EVERY arriving page: the list now silently walks the entire server dataset page-by-page with no user scroll. Worse, that inflated `currentPage` is then amplified by `refreshLoadedPages`, which fires `pagesLoaded` requests IN PARALLEL on every `useFocusEffect` bump — so a search session that auto-paged to page 12 makes every subsequent focus a 12-request parallel burst. Bounded for Conversations (perPage 100), but UniversalList is the shared list for every screen and any future `filterItems` consumer at perPage 20 inherits it. FIX: cap consecutive auto-fetches that were not preceded by a user scroll (e.g. an `autoPageCountRef` reset on scroll), and/or chunk `refreshLoadedPages` instead of `Promise.all` over every loaded page. | [LOW — CONFIRMED] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/lib/animation/AnimatedPressable.tsx:16-36 — `entering?: any; exiting?: any;` is justified by a comment claiming "Reanimated's exact builder union type isn't re-exported from the package's top-level entry point". That is factually wrong for the installed version: react-native-reanimated 4.1.7 exports it publicly — `node_modules/react-native-reanimated/lib/typescript/index.d.ts:25`: `export type { AnimatedProps, EntryOrExitLayoutType }`. FIX: `import type { EntryOrExitLayoutType } from "react-native-reanimated"` and type both props as `EntryOrExitLayoutType`; delete the incorrect justification so it doesn't get copy-pasted as precedent for `any`. | [LOW — PLAUSIBLE, needs device check] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/SearchBar.tsx:146-162 with AnimatedPressable.tsx:51-79 — the entering/exiting layout animation is now on the SAME node whose `useAnimatedStyle` already writes `opacity` (1 → 0.85 on press-in). Reanimated layout animations and an animated style that drive the same property on one view fight each other; the FadeIn/FadeOut may not play (X pops in/out) or the view can settle at the press-in opacity. The Jest test can only assert prop presence, not the visual. Also note `margin: -14` makes the 44pt box overlap the last ~14pt of the TextInput (siblings later in the tree win the touch on Android, so cursor placement at the end of the field is dead) and overhang the container's 12pt padding by 2pt (that sliver is outside the parent's bounds → still not hit-testable). FIX: verify the fade on a real Android device; if it's lost, either drive opacity solely from the animated style, or restore the wrapper but give it explicit `width: 44, height: 44, alignItems/justifyContent: center` (which fixes the original hit-test bug without collapsing the ancestor). DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §3/§9 make price the second hero and name the chat header as a PriceTag surface. FIX: consider a `<PriceTag size="sm" />` on the row (trailing the title, or under it), and at minimum render an offer preview at `fontWeight: '700'` / `colors.foreground` even when the thread is read so a live negotiation is scannable.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): src/screens/chat/Conversations.tsx offers an inbox/archived tab pair and an all/unread/read chip filter, but there is NO way to search. Once a user has a dozen threads the only way to find 'the buyer about the iPhone' is to scroll. Chat is where every Hatiwal deal happens, so this is a core findability gap. (Mobile already ships in-thread message search — TASK-N803 — so this is the missing list-level half.)

BUILD (mobile only; no API change):
1. New shared src/components/common/SearchBar.tsx — RTL-aware muted container + lucide Search icon + RNR `Input` (src/components/reusables/input) + optional clear X + optional debounceMs, colors via useColors() inline styles (never className for colors), plus SearchBar.stories.tsx covering empty / typed / clear / RTL / dark. This is deliberately the `<SearchBar>` half of open refactor ticket R15 in docs/REFACTOR_DUPLICATION.md so this feature does not hand-roll a third copy of the search row; R15's FilterChip/ChipScrollRow/CategoryChips work stays untouched.
2. Retrofit the existing browse search row (src/screens/buyer/browse/BrowseHeader.tsx, the muted container + Search icon + Input block) to use <SearchBar> with its current 400ms server debounce and animated clear button — rendered output must stay visually identical.
3. Add <SearchBar> above the tab/filter rows in Conversations.tsx (instant client-side, clear X). Extract the predicate into src/screens/chat/conversations/filterConversations.ts: case-insensitive, trimmed match against otherParticipant?.name (falling back to buyer?.name / seller?.name), listing?.title, and lastMessageBody. Feed the term into the existing makeFetcher(tabMode, filter, term) client-side filter and include it in listConfig.id (debounced ~250ms) so UniversalList re-renders without a skeleton flash. It must compose with — not replace — the archived tab and the all/unread/read filter, and must not affect the unread badge (badge stays derived from the unfiltered allConversationsRef).
4. No-match state via UniversalList's existing empty props: new `chat.search.*` keys (placeholder, noMatchTitle with the term, noMatchDescription, clearSearch) added to all 3 locales (en, ps, fa), with a Clear-search emptyAction.

WEB MIRROR (follow-up card, not this one): the same client-side filter over getConversations() in hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/conversations-view.tsx, which has the identical gap.

TESTS (mandatory per CLAUDE.md): Jest unit for filterConversations (name / title / last-message hits, case-insensitivity, whitespace-only term = no filtering, null listing and null lastMessageBody safe) in src/screens/chat/conversations/__tests__/; Storybook stories for SearchBar; Maestro flow maestro/chat/conversations-search.yaml covering happy path (type → list narrows), no-match (empty state + Clear search restores the full list), and search combined with the unread filter.
- **Acceptance**: 1. Typing on the Conversations screen narrows the list live to threads whose counterpart name, listing title, or last-message preview contains the term (case-insensitive, trimmed); clearing it restores the full list with no skeleton flash. 2. Search composes with the existing controls: it filters within the Archived tab, and search + 'Unread' shows only unread matches. 3. A term with no matches shows the localized no-match empty state naming the term plus a Clear-search action that empties the field. 4. The header/tab unread badge and the chat-store unread total are unchanged by searching (still computed from the unfiltered inbox). 5. Archive / mark-read / mark-unread / delete still work on a filtered row and keep their optimistic behaviour + rollback. 6. src/components/common/SearchBar.tsx exists and is used by BOTH Conversations.tsx and BrowseHeader.tsx; BrowseHeader keeps its 400ms debounce and animated clear and looks unchanged; grep shows no remaining hand-rolled search-input block in either screen. 7. All new strings exist in en, ps and fa — no hardcoded strings; no hardcoded colors (useColors() inline styles only, no className for color). 8. RTL verified on ps and fa: icon, input text alignment and clear X mirror correctly. 9. Light and dark both correct. 10. Jest suite (filter predicate) passes, SearchBar stories render in all states, and maestro/chat/conversations-search.yaml passes.

Then extend coverage: e2e/mock-api/server.mjs has no `sold_listings` route today (all its fixtures are `images: []`, line 149), so add the route and assert absence on /en/sellers/1 → Sold; if the surface stays untestable, the comment, the spec comment and the migration note must say "/bazaar" instead of "the public feed". | NIT — typography off the house scale, and the one place the 10px hurts. /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/remote-image.tsx:68 uses `text-[10px]`, but DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §3 bottoms the scale out at `text-xs` for meta, and mobile's two equivalents are both 11px (hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/ListingCard.tsx:404 and screens/seller/my-listings/SellerListingCard.tsx:176, `fontSize: 11, color: colors.mutedForeground`) — so the web caption is the smallest text in the app and smaller than the thing it claims parity with. It also lands the only sub-AA pairing: `text-muted-foreground` on `bg-muted` computes ≈4.33:1 in light mode (hsl(215 16% 47%) on hsl(210 40% 96%)), fine at ≈5.7:1 in dark; the pairing itself is house-standard (ui/avatar.tsx:41, ui/badge.tsx:20) but those are `text-sm`, where the ratio is not being asked to carry 10px glyphs. FIX: `text-xs`. It stays inside the tightest tile — "No photo" is ~54px and the ps/fa strings ~60px inside the ~88px content box of the 96px `list` thumbnail (w-24, minus `px-1`) — so the existing `truncate` still never fires at the narrow end, and the grid tile has room to spare. | NIT — the parity claim is backwards and invites a future "fix". The commit message, listing-card.tsx:88-92 and the MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION C2-* entry all justify the caption as "mobile's SellerListingCard states it in words" — true, but incomplete: mobile's SHARED card captions photoless listings unconditionally (hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/ListingCard.tsx:393-405 — no owner gate, and its own test asserts the label on a plain `makeListing({thumbnailUrl: null})`), so the buyer feed on mobile is captioned too. Suppressing it on web's public feed is therefore a deliberate DIVERGENCE, not parity, and nothing in the code says so — the next parity sweep will read mobile, read the web comment, and "restore" the caption to /bazaar. FIX: mark it the way the kebab is already marked in account/seller-listing-actions.tsx ("a DELIBERATE deviation from mobile … do not 'restore parity'"), one line in the ListingCard comment plus the migration entry. | VERIFIED GOOD (no action) — the rest of the card and the screen around it hold up against the eight checks. Hierarchy: photo still leads (aspect-square, `CARD_IMAGE_SIZES` per track set), PriceTag `size="md"` stays the loudest text, and the footer primary is `font-semibold sm:text-sm` so it does not undercut it. Library compliance: no hand-rolled primitives — Button, DropdownMenu, Badge, Skeleton from ui/, and PriceTag/StatusBadge/ExpiryBadge/EmptyState/SegmentedControl from shared/; the six status filters really are the shared control now (segmented-control.tsx `wrap` variant, `role="tablist"`/`tab`/`aria-selected`, `min-h-10`, focus-visible ring), not the old 32px pills. RTL: `dir` on the Radix menu, logical `end-2`/`px`/`pe-12` throughout, and the new caption is direction-neutral (flex-col centred). Dark: tokens only in the diff (`bg-muted`, `text-muted-foreground`), no hex. States: skeleton (`ListingGridSkeleton withFooter` reserves the 40px action row so the grid does not reflow on load), filtered vs virgin empty states, and a retry-wired EmptyState on error with the filter row and the "{count} listings" line both correctly gated on real `data`. Targets: `h-auto min-h-10` primary that wraps instead of clipping, `size-10` kebab, `min-h-10 py-2` menu items. Trust/a11y: `listing.detail.actionFor`/`moreOptionsFor` carry the listing title so six identical buttons are distinguishable, and `role="img"` pruning keeps the tile's accessible name the listing title (spec asserts it). Also confirmed matchesTab (seller-listings-view.tsx:42-51) routes Active/Expired through the shared `listingExpiryState()` rather than the stale server flag. The one remaining information gap — a reserved/sold card says "Reserved" but never for WHOM — is already tracked as an open MED item in /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/docs/MOBILE_WEB_PARITY.md ("Thicker seller list cards"), blocked on a `sale` field in the listing payload, so it is correctly out of scope here.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): on web, /my-listings (src/components/account/seller-listings-view.tsx) renders the generic <ListingGrid hrefFor={l => `/my-listings/${l.id}`}> with no per-card actions. A seller who wants to publish a draft, mark an item sold, reserve it, renew an expired listing, unpublish, or delete must open /my-listings/[id], act, then navigate back — once per listing. Mobile already does all of this inline (src/screens/seller/my-listings/SellerListingCard.tsx owns a renewListing mutation + confirmAlert). Sellers are the scarce side of this marketplace; this is the roughest friction left in the web dashboard.

BUILD (web only; mobile already has it):
1. Extract the shared lifecycle brain out of src/components/account/manage-listing-view.tsx into a new src/components/account/listing-actions.tsx: move the existing `LIFECYCLE` label/success/confirm-key map, `actionsFor(status, expired)` (draft→publish; reserved→sold + [activate]; active+expired→renew + [sold]; active→sold + [reserve, unpublish, renew]; sold→none) and a `useListingLifecycle()` hook wrapping `listingLifecycle`/`deleteMyListing` from src/lib/api/me.ts (toast via the existing `listing.*Success` keys, `qc.invalidateQueries` on ['my-listings'] and ['my-listing', id], busy state, error toast). manage-listing-view.tsx must then IMPORT them — the map and resolver must exist in exactly one file (house rule 1, no duplication).
2. Extend, do NOT fork, the shared card: add an optional `footer?: React.ReactNode` prop to src/components/shared/listing-card.tsx (rendered under the card body in both grid and list variants) and an optional `footerFor?: (listing) => React.ReactNode` to src/components/shared/listing-grid.tsx.
3. New src/components/account/seller-listing-actions.tsx: the footer action row — one primary Button (label from LIFECYCLE via actionsFor) plus a kebab using the existing src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx holding the secondary actions + Edit (link to /listings/[id]/edit) + Delete (destructive). Reuse src/components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx for every non-buyer action, and reuse the EXISTING src/components/account/sell-buyer-dialog.tsx (SellBuyerDialog) for reserve/sold plus src/components/shared/review-prompt-dialog.tsx when the returned transaction has a buyer — identical behaviour to /my-listings/[id].
4. Wire it in seller-listings-view.tsx via `footerFor`.

ENDPOINTS (no change, all already proxy-allow-listed in src/app/api/me/[...path]/route.ts): PUT /api/me/my/listings/:id/{publish,unpublish,reserve,activate,sold,renew}, DELETE /api/me/my/listings/:id. No new API fields, no serializer change. No new i18n keys expected (all listing.* action/confirm/success keys already exist in messages/{en,ps,fa}.json); if any is added it goes into all 3 locales.
- **Acceptance**: 1. On /my-listings every card shows a primary action matching actionsFor(): draft→Publish, active→Mark sold, active+expired→Renew, reserved→Mark sold, sold→no PRIMARY (amended after two review cycles read this as "no action row": a sold listing keeps its row, because Edit and Delete must still be reachable inline — the kebab simply takes the whole row and carries the "More options" label instead of being a lone glyph); the kebab holds exactly the remaining secondary actions plus Edit and Delete. 2. Mark sold / Reserve open SellBuyerDialog; choosing a buyer records the Transaction and immediately shows ReviewPromptDialog — same as on /my-listings/[id]. 3. Every other action shows the existing confirm dialog (existing listing.confirm* copy) and a success toast (existing listing.*Success copy); Delete is destructive-styled and removes the card. 4. After any action the grid and the per-status tab counts update with no page reload and no skeleton flash (['my-listings'] invalidated); a failed action shows an error toast and leaves the card unchanged. 5. grep shows `actionsFor` and the LIFECYCLE map defined in exactly ONE file, imported by both manage-listing-view.tsx and seller-listing-actions.tsx; listing-card.tsx is extended (new optional prop) and not forked or copied. 6. Buttons/menu are disabled while a mutation is in flight (no double-submit). 7. /ps and /fa: action row and dropdown align to the start/end correctly in RTL; light and dark both readable (tokens only, no hex). 8. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` are clean.

## TASK-WEB-B3-HISTORY
- **Title**: Recent-searches memory on the web search fields (header + Bazaar sidebar) — mobile parity
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: MEDIUM · correctness — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/layout/header-search.tsx:122-127. The new DIRTY guard drops a published term PERMANENTLY, not temporarily. The adopt effect re-runs only when `committed` changes, and `publishBrowseQuery` now dedupes by value, so a publication skipped because the field was dirty is never re-examined once the field goes clean again. Failure scenario (lg+, /bazaar, both fields on screen, header lastPushed=""): the buyer types "iph" in the header; within the 350ms window the Bazaar's already-pending debounce settles and commits "sony" → publish("sony") → header skips it (dirty); the buyer then erases "iph" back to "" before their own debounce fires, so the debounce effect at line 131 early-returns (`value.trim() === lastPushed.current`) and the header never publishes either. End state: the header field is EMPTY while the feed is filtered by "sony" and the sidebar shows "sony" — precisely the lie `use-browse-query.ts` exists to prevent, and a regression versus the pre-change behavior (which adopted "sony", at the cost of clobbering the typing). Fix: make the effect re-run on the dirty→clean EDGE instead of only on publication — `const dirty = value.trim() !== lastPushed.current;` and deps `[committed, dirty]`, keeping the `if (dirty) return;` guard. That does not reintroduce the popstate re-adopt the author was avoiding: the popstate sync leaves the field clean both before and after (it moves `value` and `lastPushed` together), so `dirty` never transitions and the effect does not re-run. Alternative: stash the skipped term in a ref and drain it from the debounce effect's no-op branch. | LOW · test-strictness — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/e2e/search-history.spec.ts:839-841. The "tightened, exact, no tolerance" overflow assertion still carries scrollbar-width slack, for exactly the reason the sibling `insideViewport` check was just fixed at line 573-579: `document.documentElement.scrollWidth` excludes the classic scrollbar Chromium renders on the (long) /bazaar page at 375px, while `window.innerWidth` includes it, so the difference sits at about -15 with zero overflow and the `<= 0` bound tolerates ~15px of REAL horizontal overflow. Failure scenario: a future change makes a chip or the panel overhang the content box by 10px at 375px — the spec still passes and the comment claims it cannot. Fix: `document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth` (same substitution already applied at line 578). | LOW · docs accuracy — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/docs/MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION.md:61. The refreshed row claims "22 Playwright specs in e2e/search-history.spec.ts"; the file contains 21 `test(` blocks (20 at HEAD + the one added here). The "23/23 green" the author observed includes the 2 fixtures in `e2e/auth.setup.ts`, which the `chromium` project depends on. The previous "20" matched the block count exactly, so the row's invariant is now broken. Fix: 22 → 21. | LOW · duplication (the house's top rule) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx:21 still hand-declares the identical surface recipe (`rounded-md border bg-popover text-popover-foreground shadow-md`) that the new `src/components/shared/floating-panel.ts` was created to centralize, and the constant's own docstring concedes it must be "kept in step" manually — i.e. the copy most likely to drift next is the one left out. Failure scenario: a future radius/shadow token change lands in `FLOATING_PANEL_SURFACE`, both hand-placed panels follow, every Radix menu on the site does not, and the drift the change set out to kill reappears between the menus and the panels. Fix: `cn(FLOATING_PANEL_SURFACE, "z-50 min-w-[10rem] overflow-hidden p-1", …)` in DropdownMenuContent (surface-only import, so no positioning/z-index leaks either way). | NIT · responsiveness — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/search-history-panel.tsx:101. Dropping `100vw` was right, but gating the widening at `lg` over-corrects: between `md` and `lg` the header's bar field is roughly 300px, so the panel is now ~300px where it used to be 24rem, and a 10-term history goes back to mostly one chip per row — the same readability argument the `lg` branch makes. 24rem from that field's start edge fits comfortably at ≥768px (field starts after the logo, ~100px). Consider `md:w-[max(100%,24rem)]` instead of `lg:`, or say in the comment that the tablet band is deliberately narrow. | OBSERVATION (no action) — the version-counter removal in /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/lib/use-browse-query.ts is safe in practice, and I checked the case the old docstring defended: for the store to be ahead of a field, that field must have moved `lastPushed` without publishing (the URL sync at header-search.tsx:79-89). Every such window closes because `browse-client.tsx:145-158` re-publishes `filters.q` on mount and on every filters change, and the header re-syncs on each pathname change — so on /bazaar the store always converges to the URL's `q`, and off /bazaar the `router.push` that follows a commit re-syncs the other header copy. Also worth noting the render-phase `valueRef.current = value` write is intentional here: moving it into an effect would order it after the adopt effect in the same commit and read one render stale. | RTL (MED, user-visible in 2 of 3 locales) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/search-history-panel.tsx:156. The chip label `<span className="min-w-0 truncate">{term}</span>` inherits the panel's `direction: rtl` in ps/fa, so a LATIN term is elided from its START instead of its end. Verified on a real render at /ps/bazaar and /fa/bazaar, 1280px and 375px: the term "samsung galaxy a54 128gb kabul very good condition" renders as "…galaxy a54 128gb kabul very good condition" — the brand word, the single most identifying token in an Afghan phone marketplace, is the one thing dropped. In /en the same chip reads "samsung galaxy a54 128gb kabul very good …". This is also a parity break: mobile's chip label is `numberOfLines={1}` on a RN <Text>, i.e. tail-ellipsis regardless of locale, so mobile always keeps "samsung galaxy…". FIX: add `dir="auto"` to that span — each term then lays out and elides in ITS OWN base direction. Mutation-proven in the browser: with `dir="auto"` injected on the live /ps panel the chip becomes "samsung galaxy a54 128gb kabul very good …" while a Pashto term ("موټر کرولا ۲۰۱۵ ماډل …") renders byte-identically before and after, so there is no RTL regression. Screenshots: scratchpad/shots/10-rtl-current.png vs 11-rtl-dirauto.png. (`title={term}` mitigates on desktop hover only, not on touch.) | DARK MODE / CONTRAST (MINOR) — src/components/shared/search-history-panel.tsx:170. The X half's hover (`hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-destructive` over the pill's `bg-muted`) computes to 3.04:1 in light and 2.94:1 in dark — at/below the 3:1 WCAG 1.4.11 non-text floor, and WORSE than the same glyph at rest (`text-muted-foreground` on `bg-muted` = 4.30:1 light / 5.77:1 dark). The one control whose mis-tap costs a saved search gets harder to see at the exact moment of interaction, and it is dark mode that fails. The component already documents the correct answer for the sibling half ("the label keeps `text-foreground` through the hover: the tint carries the affordance") — that reasoning was just not applied here. FIX: `hover:bg-destructive/10 hover:text-foreground` → 14.38:1 light / 13.49:1 dark, the red tint still carries "this forgets it". Alternative if a red glyph is wanted: invert the half, `hover:bg-destructive hover:text-destructive-foreground` → 3.62:1 / 4.59:1, both ≥3:1. | TARGETS (MINOR, in-scope file) — src/components/map/location-search.tsx:152. The suggestion rows are `px-3 py-2 text-sm`, which MEASURES 36px tall on a 390px viewport (Playwright, Nominatim stubbed: `[[356,36]]`) — under the 40px floor the rest of this feature was just held to (the chips all measure 44×44). Same geocoder field mobile uses, reached from the create-listing form on a phone. FIX: add `min-h-11` to that button (keeps `items-start` and the 2-line clamp intact). | TYPOGRAPHY (NIT, in-scope file) — src/components/map/location-search.tsx:168. `text-[10px]` on the OpenStreetMap attribution row: an off-scale hardcoded font size (measured computed 10px) in a codebase whose rule is token classes only, and 10px in Arabic script (ps/fa) is effectively unreadable. FIX: `text-xs`. Everything in the changed panel itself is clean on this axis (`text-xs` heading + Clear all, `text-sm` chips, no inline sizes). | TEST COVERAGE (NIT) — e2e/search-history.spec.ts:429 "renders right-to-left in Pashto" asserts only `html[dir=rtl]` plus chip visibility, so it would pass unchanged if `absolute start-0` regressed to `left-0` and the panel detached from the field in RTL. I verified the mirroring by hand at /ps/bazaar 1280px: panel box [880,1264] vs field box [1014,1264] — shared RIGHT edge, correct. Worth pinning as one assertion (panel.right ≈ field.right in ps, panel.left ≈ field.left in en) since RTL anchoring is the axis this panel is most likely to lose silently. | OBSERVATION (follow-up card, not blocking) — "Clear all" wipes the whole history with no confirm, no toast and no undo, and the panel then unmounts, so a screen-reader user gets no confirmation either. Mobile's `clearHistory` behaves identically, so PARITY IS CORRECT as shipped and I would not change it in this task; but sonner is already in the stack, so an undo toast (3 new keys) is the cheap improvement for both clients. Same for a removed chip: it disappears silently. | CONFIRMED GOOD (measured, not assumed) — (1) Targets: every chip label, every X and "Clear all" measure 44px tall, and all ten X boxes measure 44×44 at BOTH 1280px (sidebar field, 384px panel) and 375px (header field) with a 50-char term present; the `shrink-0` fix holds. (2) Focus: the ring hugs each pill half with no page-coloured offset strip cutting through the pill — visually confirmed via ArrowDown at 375px (shots/05). (3) Dark mode: all-token (`bg-popover`/`text-popover-foreground`/`bg-muted`/`text-muted-foreground`), no hex anywhere in the diff; verified rendering in en-light, en-dark, ps-light, fa-dark. Heading 4.72:1 light / 6.67:1 dark, "Clear all" 5.20 / 4.72, chip label 16.31 / 14.16 — all pass. (4) Hover grammar works as documented: label half tints primary/10, X half tints destructive/10, each filling exactly its own mirrored half (shots/07, 08). (5) No horizontal overflow at 375px (documentElement scrollWidth - clientWidth = 0) and the panel stays inside the content box. (6) Library compliance / no duplication: `src/components/ui/` has NO popover or command primitive, so the hand-placed panel is the correct call rather than a Radix rebuild; a grep for hand-rolled floating surfaces across src returns exactly two hits (search-history-panel.tsx:101, location-search.tsx:142) and both now import FLOATING_PANEL_SURFACE — no third drifted copy was missed. Chips are built from the shared `Button`. (7) Clipping/stacking hold up: the Bazaar SearchBox sits OUTSIDE the sidebar's `lg:overflow-y-auto` filter card, and `aside lg:z-30` under `header z-40` is right. (8) i18n: all four `browse.*` keys present in en/ps/fa with the `{term}` interpolation intact.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (verified in code): mobile remembers a buyer's last 10 search terms (src/stores/searchHistory.store.ts, chips in BrowseHeader.tsx with per-term X + Clear all). The web client has NO search memory at all — src/components/layout/header-search.tsx and the Bazaar sidebar search in src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx start empty on every visit. The translations are ALREADY shipped and unused: `browse.recentSearches` and `browse.clearHistory` exist in en/ps/fa while grep for `recentSearches` in src returns nothing. BUILD: 1. New src/lib/use-search-history.ts — localStorage key `hatiwal.searchHistory`, mobile store rules (trim, ignore <2 chars, dedupe, most-recent-first, cap 10), exposing { history, add, remove, clear }; SSR-safe with hydration inside useEffect and try/catch around storage. 2. New src/components/shared/search-history-panel.tsx — heading with a History icon, removable chip per term, clear-all action, tokens only, RTL-aware. 3. Wire BOTH entry points (header-search.tsx and the browse-client sidebar form): add(term) on commit, panel shown only when focused + empty + history non-empty; chip click applies via the existing go()/update({ q }) path; Escape/blur closes. No API change, no new i18n keys, works for guests; never send history to the server or the URL.
- **Acceptance**: 1. Search 'iphone' from the header, then focus the empty header field: 'Recent searches' with an 'iphone' chip appears; the same history appears on the Bazaar sidebar field (one shared store). 2. Reload the page → the chip is still there; clicking it navigates/filters to /bazaar?q=iphone. 3. The X on a chip removes only that term; 'Clear all' empties the list and the panel disappears. 4. The panel never shows while the field has text, and never when history is empty. 5. Cap and hygiene: an 11th search drops the oldest, repeating a term moves it to the front without duplicating, a 1-character term is never stored. 6. Works logged out; nothing search-history-related is sent to Rails and no term is added to the URL beyond the existing ?q=. 7. With localStorage unavailable the page still renders and search still works. 8. /ps and /fa RTL correct; light and dark readable. 9. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

## TASK-WEB-OWN947
- **Title**: Web: owner view of your own listing is a dead end — add an owner action bar to /listings/[id]
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
