- **ReviewNotes**: [MED] RTL/i18n truncation still open for fa — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/owner-listing-bar.tsx:191-236. The `min-w-40 flex-1` floor shifts the clipping window instead of closing it: 2-up splits as soon as the panel's inner width reaches 328px (viewport >= ~392px), and the fa label needs ~184px of button. Measured on the running app: fa @412px scrollWidth 96 / clientWidth 84 (CLIPPED), @430px 96/93 (CLIPPED), clean again at 480px; the screenshot shows "مشاهده گفت…" — the verb is eaten, exactly the defect the last review raised. en (72px) and ps (72px) pass at every width, which is why the 375px en/ps spec is green. FIX: adopt the house pattern already used for this same problem in account/seller-listing-actions.tsx — `h-auto min-h-10 whitespace-normal` on both buttons of the row and drop `truncate` from the labels, so a long label wraps inside a 40px-floor button instead of disappearing (alternatively `min-w-48`, which pushes the split to ~456px, or a deterministic `grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2`). Then extend the clipping spec in e2e/listing-detail.spec.ts:547 to loop fa as well as en/ps and to include 412 and 430 — fa is the binding locale here, and the two widths in the current loop are both on the safe side of the split. | [MED] New `count` badge variant fails AA in dark mode — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/ui/badge.tsx:17 (used by owner-listing-bar.tsx:222 and layout/auth-nav.tsx:69). Measured after transitions settle: dark `--primary` composites to rgb(60,131,246) with rgb(255,255,255) text = 3.64:1 at 12px, below the 4.5:1 AA floor; light mode is rgb(36,99,235) = 4.88:1 and passes. The variant's own comment rejects the tinted `default` for being ~3.6:1, so the replacement lands in the same place in the theme half of users read at night — and this is the one number the panel exists to make a seller act on. FIX: add `dark:bg-primary/80` to the variant (composites to ~5.1:1 over the dark background) or introduce a dedicated darker count token in globals.css; assert the ratio in a spec so the next palette tweak can't silently re-break it. | [MED] The panel's loudest control is the irreversible transition, and it diverges from mobile — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/account/listing-actions.tsx:120-123. For a plain active listing web returns `primary: "sold"`; mobile's shared hook (hatiwal-mobile/src/hooks/useListingLifecycle.ts:323-335, the same hook SellerListingCard uses) returns `markReserved` for active and keeps `sold` in moreActions — so the comment at listing-actions.tsx:96-97 ("Mirrors mobile's SellerListingCard") is inaccurate for the majority status. This card is what raises the stakes: the filled primary now sits at the top of the seller's own PUBLIC listing page, the surface they land on from their own share link or a search result, and `sold` is terminal in this brain (`actionsFor` returns `{secondary: []}`, and web has no duplicate/relist — it is still an open item in docs/MOBILE_WEB_PARITY.md), so the most emphatic button on the page is the one with no path back. The confirm + buyer picker make a mis-tap recoverable, but the emphasis is on the wrong step of the real-world flow (reserve while meeting the buyer, then sold). FIX: return `{ primary: "reserve", secondary: ["sold", "unpublish", "renew"] }` for a non-lapsed active listing, matching mobile, and update the /my-listings + listing-detail specs; if the divergence is deliberate, say so in the comment the way the kebab-vs-labelled-More deviation is documented in seller-listing-actions.tsx. | [LOW] The panel warns about an expiry it cannot act on — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/owner-listing-bar.tsx:150 with 156-168. `<ExpiryBadge>` renders the amber "Expires tomorrow" / "Expires in N days" state, but the panel renders only `primary` and discards `secondary`, and for a not-yet-lapsed active listing `renew` lives in `secondary` — so the pill states urgency whose fix is one navigation away. That is the same "names a status it can't fix" complaint that justified wiring Renew for the already-expired case. FIX: when `listingExpiryState(...).kind === "soon"`, render Renew as an extra outline button in the first row (it is already a legal transition per actionsFor), or promote it to primary in the soon state as it is in the expired state. | [LOW] Two count pills, two digit systems in ps/fa — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/layout/auth-nav.tsx:73 renders `{unread > 9 ? "9+" : unread}` raw, while the panel's pill (owner-listing-bar.tsx:223) goes through `formatNumber(conversationsCount, locale)` and shows ۲. Since this card deliberately merged both pills onto the same primitive, they should also agree on numerals. FIX: `formatNumber(unread, locale)` in auth-nav (cap included, e.g. format 9 then append the localized plus, or use an i18n key for the overflow label). | [LOW] `src/components/ui/card.tsx` is dead code while three surfaces on this page hand-roll it — grep for `@/components/ui/card` across /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src returns zero importers, yet owner-listing-bar.tsx:134 (`rounded-lg border border-primary/30 bg-primary/10 p-4`) and app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx:212 and :239 (`rounded-lg border bg-card p-4`) each re-declare the same surface recipe. AGENTS.md rule 1 says extend the shared primitive, never re-implement it. FIX: either render these three as `<Card className="...">` (the tint stays a className on the panel) so the radius/border/shadow recipe lives in one file, or delete ui/card.tsx and stop shipping an unused primitive that invites exactly this drift. | [LOW/nit] On a phone the panel is four stacked equal-width 40px buttons (~180px of chrome) above the location card, which flattens the emphasis the primary is supposed to own — see the 393px screenshot. FIX (optional, design call): keep the lifecycle primary and Manage as buttons and demote "View Chats" to a single link row carrying its count (it is navigation, not an action), or pair Edit + Manage 2-up once the label-wrap fix from finding 1 makes a narrow row safe.
- **Description**: ## The bug (verified in code) On `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx` an ACTIVE listing renders `StartConversationButton`, `SellerPhoneReveal`, `SaveButton`, `HideListingButton` and `ReportButton` — and every single one returns `null` for the listing's own seller (`start-conversation-button.tsx:57`, `seller-phone-reveal.tsx:28`, `save-button.tsx:59`, `hide-listing-button.tsx:27`, `report-button.tsx:55`). Since `isActive` is true, the `else` notice branch never renders either. So a seller who opens their own listing (from their own share link, Google, a category page, the similar rail) gets an EMPTY action column whose only surviving element is `<SafetyTips>` — buyer 'how to meet safely' guidance shown to the seller about their own item. `listing.detail.ownListingNotice` already exists in all three web locale files and is never rendered anywhere. ## Build Create `hatiwal-web/src/components/listing/owner-listing-bar.tsx` (`"use client"`, uses `useAuth()` from `@/components/auth/auth-provider` since the page is a Server Component). Props: `listingId`, `sellerId`, `status`, `expiresAt`, `expired`, `viewsCount`, `savesCount`. Returns `null` unless `status === "authed" && user?.id === sellerId` — so buyers see zero change. When it IS the owner, render one bordered `bg-card` panel: - Heading row: `listing.detail.ownListingNotice` (reuse the existing key) + `<StatusBadge status={status}/>` + `<ExpiryBadge status expiresAt expired/>` (reuse `@/components/shared/expiry-badge`, already gates on `status === "active"` and >7 days). - Buttons: primary `Link href={"/my-listings/" + listingId}` → `listing.management` / manage (the existing `ManageListingView` with publish/reserve/sold/renew/delete + views chart); outline `Link href={"/listings/" + listingId + "/edit"}` → `common.edit`; ghost `Link href={"/conversations?listing=" + listingId}` → the buyer conversations for this listing (`/conversations` already reads `?listing=` — `conversations/page.tsx:21-31`). - Use `@/i18n/navigation` `Link` (never `next/link`) so the locale prefix is kept. Wire it into `page.tsx`: place it directly above the `isActive ? … : …` action block, and pass `ownerId={listing.seller?.id}` to `<SafetyTips>` so it hides for the owner (add the same `ownerId`/`useAuth` guard already used by `HideListingButton`). ## API No endpoint or contract change. `GET /listings/:id` already returns `expiresAt`, `expired`, `viewsCount`, `savesCount` (`src/lib/types.ts:141-142`). ## i18n Reuse `listing.detail.ownListingNotice`, `listing.management`, `common.edit`. Add only what is genuinely new (e.g. `listing.detail.ownerViewConversations`) to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`. ## Mobile mirror (note, not this task) Mobile `src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx:906-926` shows a bare `ownListingNotice` banner with no Manage/Edit action either — the same bar should later be added there; do NOT build it in this card.
- **Acceptance**: 1) Signed in as the seller, open your own ACTIVE listing at /en/listings/<id>: an owner panel shows the 'this is your listing' notice, the status badge, the expiry pill when <=7 days out, and three working links (Manage → /my-listings/<id>, Edit → /listings/<id>/edit, Conversations → /conversations?listing=<id>). 2) SafetyTips no longer renders for the owner. 3) Signed in as any OTHER user, and as a guest, the page is byte-for-byte unchanged (no owner panel, Message/Offer/Save/Report/SafetyTips all still render). 4) Owner view also works for draft/reserved/sold/expired: the panel renders, ExpiryBadge stays hidden for non-active. 5) All new strings exist in en/ps/fa; verified in /ps and /fa with correct RTL button order and in dark mode. 6) npx tsc --noEmit clean and npm run build passes.

## TASK-WEB-REP815
- **Title**: Web: surface your own rating + reviews on /profile (currently invisible) with a link to your public profile
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-3 (dev DONE — all six cycle-2 design findings fixed on /profile rating+reviews: 40px hit box, sr-only accessible name, deduped score on /sellers/[id]; committed f1da6fd then folded into 41b133c). BOTH REVIEWS ARE OWED — code review and design review both died in the session limit. Re-review only; do NOT rebuild.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code)
`hatiwal-web/src/components/account/profile-view.tsx` (read in full) shows three stat tiles (active/sold/saved), info rows, and four quick-action buttons. There is **no rating, no review count, no way to read the reviews buyers left about you, and no link to your own public profile**. A seller on web literally cannot see their own reputation — the one number that drives whether buyers message them. Mobile `src/screens/shared/Profile.tsx:292` has a 'Reviews' quick action → `AllReviews` with buyer/seller tabs, and shows the rating in the header.

The data is already there and unused: `UserSerializer` `:me` view serves `avg_rating` + `review_count` (`hatiwal-api/app/serializers/user_serializer.rb:60-61`), and `hatiwal-web/src/lib/types.ts:56-57` already declares `avgRating`/`reviewCount` on `User`. Only the UI is missing.

## Build
Edit `hatiwal-web/src/components/account/profile-view.tsx`:
1. Put `<RatingDisplay avgRating={user.avgRating} reviewCount={user.reviewCount} size="lg"/>` (reuse `@/components/shared/rating-display`) under the `UserIdentity` block. It already renders a graceful 'no reviews yet' state when `avgRating` is null.
2. Add a 'View my public profile' secondary button to the existing quick-actions grid → `Link href={"/sellers/" + user.id}` (that route is the canonical public profile; `/users/[id]` redirects to it). This closes the current dead end where a seller cannot see how buyers see them.
3. Render the reviews list on your own profile by reusing `@/components/seller/reviews-section` — `<ReviewsSection sellerId={user.id} avgRating={user.avgRating} reviewCount={user.reviewCount ?? 0}/>`. It already has the of_seller/of_buyer `SegmentedControl`, TanStack Query load-more, skeleton and `EmptyState`, and `getUserReviews` is a public endpoint so it works for your own id unchanged.
4. Add an optional `title` prop to `reviews-section.tsx` (default = current `t("reviews.sectionTitle")`) and pass `reviews.myReviewsTitle` from the profile so the heading reads 'My reviews' instead of 'Ratings & reviews'. Do not fork the component.

## API
No endpoint or contract change. Reuses `GET /users/:id/reviews?role=of_seller|of_buyer` (already in `src/lib/api/reviews.ts`) and the existing `/api/me` payload.

## i18n
Add `reviews.myReviewsTitle` (mirror the mobile key/wording verbatim) and one key for the public-profile button (e.g. `profile.viewPublicProfile`) to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`. `reviews.myReviewsTitle` is currently missing from all three web locale files.
- **Acceptance**: 1) /en/profile shows your star rating + review count under your name, and a 'My reviews' section with the As-seller / As-buyer toggle, load-more, skeleton while loading, and the empty state when you have none. 2) A brand-new account with zero reviews shows the graceful no-rating state and the reviews empty state — never 'NaN', '0 stars' or a blank block. 3) A 'View my public profile' button navigates to /sellers/<your id> and back. 4) /sellers/[id] is visually unchanged (ReviewsSection default title still 'Ratings & reviews' — verified by loading another seller's page). 5) reviews.myReviewsTitle + the new profile key exist in en/ps/fa; checked in /ps and /fa (RTL star order, RTL segmented control) and in dark mode. 6) npx tsc --noEmit clean and npm run build passes.

## TASK-WEB-CAT623
- **Title**: Web: categories hub has no listing counts and no subcategories — wire ?with_counts=true and add drill-down
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-4: dev re-reviewed with no rebuild and its own verdict was APPROVE — all 16 hub counts verified against the DB via rails runner (16/16 match the rendered HTML), roll-up proven, 11/11 e2e and 30/30 backend specs green. Both independent reviews still found a live defect: CR MED categories/page.tsx:168 the subcategory block is gated on visibleSubcategories.length>0, but all 39 subcategories have 0 browsable listings, so NO chips render in the live app — gate on subcategories.length>0 instead, keep stocked-first and the 4-chip cap, and pass tone='empty' as [slug]/page.tsx:102 does (this means acceptance #2 is unmet live) | LOW api categories_controller.rb:34 use policy_scope(...).not_hidden_for(current_user), and :31 roll up over ALL children | INFO listing.rb:43 by_category now rolls up for every client including mobile. DR: the same drill-down-invisible finding | MED [slug]/page.tsx:90-110 dead "No listings" chips above the grid | LOW back-link 20px vs 44px, aria-current, revalidate 600 → 60, clamp the name | INFO e2e/mock-api/server.mjs:263-272 gives subcategories direct listings, which HIDES this bug from the E2E suite.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code) `hatiwal-web/src/app/[locale]/categories/page.tsx` calls `getCategories({revalidate:600})` and renders a bare emoji + name grid. There are **no active-listing counts and no subcategories**, so buyers cannot tell where the inventory actually is and routinely click into a category with zero items. The migration catalog claims C736 ships 'grid + live counts' — it does not; and S417 (subcategory drill-down) is genuinely unbuilt on the hub. The backend already serves everything in ONE query: `Api::V1::CategoriesController#index` with `?with_counts=true` renders the `:with_counts` view → `active_listings_count` (single GROUP BY, zero N+1) + nested `subcategories`. `hatiwal-web/src/lib/types.ts:108-113` already declares `subcategories` and the count field is unused. ## Build 1. `hatiwal-web/src/lib/api/categories.ts` — add `withCounts?: boolean` to `getCategories(opts)`; when true request `categories?with_counts=true`. Add `activeListingsCount?: number` to the `Category` interface in `src/lib/types.ts` if absent (mobile calls it `activeListingsCount` — mirror the mobile contract exactly). 2. `src/app/[locale]/categories/page.tsx` — fetch with `withCounts: true` (keep `revalidate = 600`; counts are cheap and stale-by-a-minute is fine). On each card add a muted count line using the existing `listing.shopCount` plural key. Sort/dim empty categories: a category with `activeListingsCount === 0` renders at reduced opacity with the count line reading the existing `categoriesPage.empty` copy, so buyers stop tapping into nothing. 3. Subcategory drill-down on the hub: for a category with `subcategories?.length`, render its children as small chip `Link`s to `/categories/<child.slug>` inside the card (or in a `<details>`-style expandable row below it). `/categories/[slug]` already handles subcategory chips (`categories/[slug]/page.tsx:55-63`), so children just need to become reachable from the hub. 4. Bazaar sidebar: `src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx:341` lists top-level categories only. Nest each category's `subcategories` as indented entries under the selected parent, calling the existing `update({ categorySlug: child.slug })` — `filtersToQuery` already resolves any slug (parent or child) to a `categoryId`, so no filter-logic change is needed. ## API No endpoint or contract change. `GET /categories?with_counts=true` already exists and is guest-accessible. ## i18n Reuse `listing.shopCount`, `categoriesPage.empty`, `categoriesPage.allIn`. Add at most one new key (e.g. `categoriesPage.subcategories`) to `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json`.
- **Acceptance**: 1) /en/categories shows an active-listing count on every card, sourced from ?with_counts=true — verified against the DB for at least two categories. 2) A category with zero active listings is visibly de-emphasised and says so instead of showing a bare '0'. 3) Categories that have children expose those children as tappable chips on the hub; each navigates to /categories/<child-slug> and lists that subcategory's listings. 4) The Bazaar sidebar shows subcategories nested under their parent and selecting one filters the feed to that subcategory (URL gains ?category=<child-slug>, results narrow, Clear-all resets). 5) Exactly one request is made to /categories for the hub render (no per-category count fetch) — confirm in the network tab. 6) New/reused keys present in en/ps/fa; hub and sidebar checked in /ps and /fa (RTL indentation/chip direction) and in dark mode. 7) npx tsc --noEmit clean and npm run build passes.

## TASK-O947
- **Title**: Accept offer → one-tap "Reserve for {buyer} at {price}" (no buyer picker)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: MUST-FIX — BROKEN E2E COVERAGE (rule violation). Changing `chat.offer.reserveAfterAcceptTitle` from "Reserve this listing for a buyer?" to "Reserve for {{buyerName}}?" invalidated two Maestro assertions that were never updated (`git status maestro/` shows no changes): `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/maestro/chat/offer_send_and_accept.yaml:76` — `- assertVisible: "Reserve this listing for a buyer?"` (EXACT match, no wildcards) — and `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/maestro/chat/reserve_after_accept.yaml:106` — `- assertVisible: ".*Reserve this listing for a buyer.*"`. Both now fail at the confirm step, i.e. the happy-path E2E for TASK-O947 itself is dead. This violates CLAUDE.md "Mobile Testing Rules (MANDATORY)" and mobile.prompt.md ("Any change to existing screen → Update the relevant Maestro flow"). EXTRA TRAP: a naive exact-string replacement will ALSO fail — the interpolated name is bidi-isolated, so the rendered title is `'Reserve for ⁦Ahmad Karimi⁩?'` (verified by rendering the key). FIX: use a wildcard regex in both files, e.g. `- assertVisible: ".*Reserve for.*Ahmad Karimi.*"`, and update the stale header comments in `reserve_after_accept.yaml:6` and `offer_send_and_accept.yaml:11` that still quote the old copy. | SHOULD-FIX — the pending spinner is rendered at 50% opacity, re-creating the exact ambiguity the MEDIUM/STATES fix was meant to remove. `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx:288` sets `opacity: disabled ? 0.5 : 1` on the whole `OfferActionsRow` container, and `offerActionsDisabled` is true for the TAPPED bubble too (`Conversation.tsx:1365` passes the global `isRespondingToOffer`). So the `ActivityIndicator` at MessageBubble.tsx:300/317 draws at half opacity inside a half-opacity button — on the mid-range Android the fix cites, a dimmed half-transparent spinner is barely more legible than the dead tap it replaced. The dev summary's claim that "the rest of the row stays merely dimmed" is therefore inaccurate: the pending button is dimmed as well. FIX: `opacity: disabled && !pending ? 0.5 : 1` (or move the dim to the two non-pending buttons). Also add `busy` to the a11y state — `accessibilityState={{ disabled: !!disabled, busy: pending === 'accept' }}` (MessageBubble.tsx:297, and `pending === 'decline'` at :314) — because the visible label is replaced by a spinner while `accessibilityLabel` still reads a plain "Accept", so a screen-reader user gets no progress signal at all. | SHOULD-FIX — confirm-mode content flash on every dismissal. `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx:1640-1657` drives the sheet from ONE piece of state: `visible={reserveConfirm !== null}` and `preselectedBuyer={reserveConfirm?.buyer ?? null}`. Clearing `reserveConfirm` (Not now at :1642, backdrop/X/back, and the success path at :702) flips `visible`→false AND `isConfirmMode`→false in the same commit, but `animationType="slide"` keeps the children mounted through the ~250-300 ms exit. For that whole window the sheet re-renders as the FULL pick-a-buyer view: title "Who's buying this item?" (`BuyerPickerSheet.tsx:203` falls back to `defaultTitle` once `confirmTitle` is undefined), plus the subtitle, the REV2 nudge, "No conversations yet on this listing." and the "Sold to someone not on Hatiwal" row (`BuyerPickerSheet.tsx:240-262, 331-399`). That is precisely the picker the task exists to eliminate, flashing on screen at the end of every one-tap reserve. FIX (call site): `{reserveConfirm !== null && <BuyerPickerSheet visible … />}` so it unmounts instantly, or keep the prompt in state and add a separate `confirmVisible` boolean cleared after the animation. | SHOULD-FIX — the new invalidation helper hand-rolls query keys that already exist as exported constants (duplication / drift risk; the user's stated #1 rule). `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx:370-373` hardcodes `["listing"]`, `["my-listing"]`, `["my-listings"]`, `["myListingStatusCounts"]`, but `src/hooks/useListingLifecycle.ts:53-57` already exports `MY_LISTINGS_QK`, `MY_LISTING_STATUS_COUNTS_QK`, `MY_LISTING_QK`, `CONVERSATIONS_QK` with the explicit comment "exported so callers/tests can assert against the exact same constants instead of hardcoding strings" — so a rename there now silently stops this helper from invalidating anything (no test would catch it). The helper also omits `CONVERSATIONS_QK`, which the canonical `invalidateAll()` at useListingLifecycle.ts:141-146 does invalidate (`["conversations", listingId]`, the prefix of BuyerPickerSheet's own `["conversations", listingId, "buyer-picker"]` key). FIX: import the four constants and use them; add `qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [CONVERSATIONS_QK] })` so the chat-side reserve matches the canonical lifecycle invalidation set. (Impact of the missing key is small — RQ's default `staleTime: 0` refetches on the sheet's next open — but the divergence from the one canonical set is the actual problem.) | LOW — the `["conversation", convId]` invalidation was relocated, not removed, so the NIT flagged as "folded in" (item 11) is still dead code, and the new comment now asserts something false. `grep -rn 'queryKey: ["conversation"' src/` returns only the two `invalidateQueries` CALL sites (`Conversation.tsx:362` and `:1215`) — no `useQuery` anywhere in the repo subscribes to that key, so line 362 is a guaranteed no-op. The real refresh is `load(convId)` at Conversation.tsx:374. The helper's docstring ("invalidates every query surface that needs to reflect the new status (this conversation, …)") therefore misdescribes the mechanism and will mislead the next maintainer. FIX: drop line 362 and say the conversation is refreshed by the imperative `load(convId)`. (Root cause is pre-existing and out of scope: this screen fetches `conversation`/`messages` with `useState` + manual `load()`, violating the MANDATORY React Query rule in mobile.prompt.md §12 — worth a separate card, since it is why an invalidation helper has to call an imperative loader at all.) | LOW — `Tag` is a weak "accepted" affordance and makes the new icon pair asymmetric. `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx:862` and `:1011` use `icon={offerOutcome === 'accepted' ? Tag : X}`. The dedup/distinctness goal vs. the meetup pill is met, but `Tag` is the very icon the offer bubble's own header bar already renders (MessageBubble.tsx:785), so "Offer accepted" now reuses the neutral offer glyph while "Offer declined" gets an unambiguous `X` — the success side carries no success semantics. `Check` is already imported in this file and `CheckCircle2` is already used elsewhere in the repo (useListingLifecycle.ts); either reads as "accepted" and stays distinct from `CalendarCheck`. Worth a marketplace-designer call rather than a unilateral swap. | LOW — the footer Cancel button still bypasses the new `handleDismiss` guard. `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/BuyerPickerSheet.tsx:441` is `onPress={onClose}` while `onRequestClose`, the backdrop and the header X were all routed through `handleDismiss` (:168-171). It is safe today ONLY because the Button also carries `disabled={isSubmitting}`, i.e. the mid-submit invariant now depends on two independent places agreeing. FIX: `onPress={handleDismiss}` so the guard lives in one place; the new BuyerPickerSheet.test.tsx case ("ignores the header X, the backdrop, and the hardware back button while isSubmitting") should be extended to cover the footer Cancel too. | LOW — the `OpenConfirmModeRtl` story does not actually render RTL, so INFO item 14 (visual test gap) is only partly closed. `/home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/BuyerPickerSheet.stories.tsx` (new story) hardcodes Pashto strings as props, but every mirror decision in the component reads `isRtl` from `useLocalization()` at runtime (`textAlign`, `flexDirection`, and the new `alignItems: isRtl ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start'` at BuyerPickerSheet.tsx:307). In the default Storybook locale the story therefore renders Pashto text in an LTR layout — the one thing the story is named for is the thing it cannot show; its own comment concedes you must switch the device locale. It does add real value for the long-name and null-thumbnail cases. FIX: add a decorator that forces the i18n language to `ps` for this story (the pattern the comment cites in ListingStatusBanner.stories.tsx), or rename it and leave the RTL gap open rather than marking the item done. | MUST-FIX · E2E BROKEN BY THE COPY CHANGE — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/maestro/chat/reserve_after_accept.yaml:106 and /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/maestro/chat/offer_send_and_accept.yaml:76. ISSUE: both flows assert the sheet title that item 8 removed — `assertVisible: ".*Reserve this listing for a buyer.*"` and the exact-match `assertVisible: "Reserve this listing for a buyer?"`. The title now renders "Reserve for ⁦Ahmad Karimi⁩?" so both assertions fail on the next suite run. reserve_after_accept.yaml is explicitly assigned to this task in SPRINT_BOARD.md:2180 ("broke maestro/chat/offer_in_existing_thread.yaml:17 and maestro/chat/reserve_after_accept.yaml:37 — tracked on TASK-C381 and TASK-O947"), and CLAUDE.md makes the E2E layer mandatory per feature. FIX: update both to a regex tolerant of the invisible isolate characters that now bracket the interpolated name — `.*Reserve for.*Ahmad Karimi.*` (never `"Reserve for Ahmad Karimi?"`, which cannot match across U+2066/U+2069). Also refresh the stale header comments at reserve_after_accept.yaml:6 and offer_send_and_accept.yaml:11 that still quote "Reserve for {buyer} at {price}?" as the rendered title. | MEDIUM · STATES / ERROR FEEDBACK INVISIBLE ON ANDROID — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/reserveAfterAccept.ts:216 + /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/BuyerPickerSheet.tsx (confirm-mode block, 270-323). ISSUE: the stay-open-on-failure retry contract (Conversation.tsx:694-702) deliberately keeps the sheet mounted after a failed reserve, and the ONLY failure signal is `toast.error(chat.listingActions.reserveFailed)`. The Toaster is mounted in the root view tree (app/_layout.tsx:80) and sonner-native only escapes to a FullWindowOverlay on iOS (node_modules/sonner-native/src/toaster.tsx:68-76) — on Android a raw RN <Modal> is a separate native window, so the error toast renders behind the open sheet. Net effect on the app's primary platform ("optimized for a mid-range Android", DESIGN_SYSTEM §1): spinner runs, spinner stops, sheet still open, nothing explains why — exactly the dead-tap the pending-spinner fix set out to remove. The same occlusion swallows the `chat.offer.acceptedToast` fired one tick before the sheet opens (Conversation.tsx:637 → 663), so the sheet also arrives with no acknowledgement of the accept that spawned it. FIX: give the shared sheet an inline error slot — add `errorMessage?: string` to BuyerPickerSheetProps, render it above the footer as destructive-tinted 13px text (colors.destructive, textAlign isRtl ? right : left), and set it from handleReserveAfterAcceptConfirm when reserveAfterAccept resolves false (clear it on the next confirm tap). ## TASK-WEB-SEARCHHIST
- **Title**: Web: recent-searches chips on Bazaar + header search (mobile parity, keys already translated)
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: SUPERSEDED — house-1 and house-2 built this same feature concurrently in the same tree. Closed as a duplicate; the merged implementation and the combined fix list live on TASK-WEB-B3-HISTORY. Do not pick this card up.
- **Description**: Mobile buyers get their last searches back as one-tap chips (`hatiwal-mobile/src/stores/searchHistory.store.ts` + the History block in `src/screens/buyer/browse/BrowseHeader.tsx:230-300`). Web has NOTHING — a repeat visitor must retype "Samsung A54" every session. This is a half-landed feature: `browse.recentSearches` and `browse.clearHistory` are ALREADY translated in `hatiwal-web/messages/en.json`, `ps.json` and `fa.json` with no UI consuming them, so no new i18n work beyond wiring (add only `browse.removeSearch` for the per-chip remove aria-label).

Build: ADD `hatiwal-web/src/lib/search-history.ts` — the single source for the history (localStorage key `hatiwal.browse.searchHistory`, newest-first, case-insensitive dedupe, trimmed, max 8, ignore blanks and queries under 2 chars; `read()`, `record(term)`, `remove(term)`, `clear()`; every access wrapped in try/catch so Safari private mode degrades to in-memory, matching the pattern in `src/components/chat/use-composer-draft.ts`). ADD `hatiwal-web/src/components/browse/recent-searches.tsx` — a client component rendering a `History` (lucide) label + "Clear all" on one row and horizontally-scrollable chips below, each chip = the term (click applies) plus a small X (click removes, `stopPropagation`). Rendered ONLY when the search input is empty and history is non-empty, so it never competes with live results; must sit above `SavedSearches` in the sidebar of `src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx` and use `overflow-x-auto` so it never widens the page. EDIT `src/components/browse/browse-client.tsx`: call `record()` when a search actually commits (the debounced `q` settles, not per keystroke) and pass `onApply={(term) => update({ q: term })}` so applying a chip flows through the SAME `update()` that syncs URL ⇄ filters ⇄ query (`filtersToSearchString`) — no parallel state. EDIT `src/components/layout/header-search.tsx` to `record()` on submit so searches started from the header also land in history. No API change, no Rails call, no server component touched.

MOBILE MIRROR: none needed — mobile already ships this; reuse its wording/behavior verbatim so the two clients feel identical.
- **Acceptance**: 1) On `/bazaar` with an empty search box and prior searches, a "Recent searches" row shows up to 8 chips, newest first, above Saved searches. 2) Clicking a chip fills the search box, updates the URL to `?q=<term>` and refetches — identical to typing it. 3) Typing anything hides the whole block; clearing the box brings it back. 4) The chip X removes just that term and it stays gone after a reload; "Clear all" empties the block and it disappears. 5) Searching the same term with different casing/whitespace does not create a duplicate chip, and it moves to the front. 6) A search submitted from the site header appears in the Bazaar chips. 7) Blank / 1-char queries are never recorded. 8) Works for a guest (no login required) and survives reload; with localStorage unavailable the page still renders and never throws. 9) `/ps` and `/fa`: chips right-aligned and scroll in RTL, label + "Clear all" use the existing translated keys, no clipping; verified in light and dark. 10) No horizontal page scroll at 375px width. 11) `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

EDIT `messages/en.json`, `messages/ps.json`, `messages/fa.json`: add `listing.detail.seeSimilarIn`, `listing.detail.moreFromSeller`, `listing.detail.reservedMayFreeUp` (all 3 locales; reuse the existing `soldNotice`/`reservedNotice` sentences unchanged). Add the same two actions there (router.push to Browse pre-filtered by category + price band, and to the seller profile) reusing the same key names in `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`, and keep the existing status banner as-is.

## TASK-C381
- **Title**: Make/counter an offer without leaving the chat thread (offer button in composer + buyer counter-back)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P0
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-3 (both reviews returned; the counter-chain bug and seller-initiated-offer asymmetry were fixed via a new isCounterSuperseded gate): CR MUST offerInThread.test.tsx:112-135 re-implements the guards, so it is a tautology that cannot fail — extract a pure module and test that | CR MUST maestro/chat/offer_in_existing_thread.yaml:17 the "Make an Offer" entry point no longer exists — tap composer-plus-button then composer-action-offer | CR Conversation.tsx:1177 isCounterSuperseded is a 4th full-array scan per row — useMemo the threadRows and build one index Map | CR CounterOfferSheet.tsx:197 accepts non-positive amounts — reuse Number(...)>0 plus the offerInvalid toast | DR MUST the MessageBubble outcome union needs a "countered"/isSuperseded muted chip | DR handleSendCounter has no amount validation — hoist parseOfferAmount | DR the noPaymentNote is role-coded — add chat.offer.threadNote in all 3 locales | DR only the newest pending offer should keep live actions | DR extend PriceTag with a tone prop and fold CounterOfferSheet into OfferSheet.
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
Negotiation happens in the thread, but the offer machinery is not reachable from there.

1. `OfferSheet` (`src/screens/shared/listing-detail/OfferSheet.tsx`, already carrying the TASK-G083 95/90/85% quick-amount chips) is imported ONLY by `src/screens/shared/ListingDetail.tsx`. The chat composer in `src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx` (~line 1125-1182) has Calendar (meetup), Paperclip, Image, Input, Send — and no offer affordance at all. A buyer mid-conversation must back out of the thread, reopen the listing, tap "Make an offer", which then re-runs `startConversation` and falls into the 422 duplicate-conversation fallback path just to post an offer into the thread they were already in.
2. `ListingHeader.tsx` renders a "Firm price / seller's price is firm" notice (testID `firm-price-chat-notice`) whose stated purpose is to suppress "the offer entry point in the conversation" — an entry point that does not exist.
3. Dead-end: in `Conversation.tsx` renderItem, `onOfferCounter` is passed only when `item.kind === "offer" && !isOfferCountered && isSeller`. So when the seller counters, the buyer can only accept or decline. The buyer cannot counter back, and the seller cannot open a fresh offer either — the haggle ends after exactly one round.

## Build (mobile only, no backend change)
- `src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx`
  - Add a `Tag` (lucide) icon button to the composer row, beside the existing Calendar/Paperclip/Image buttons, with `accessibilityLabel={t('chat.offer.makeOffer')}` and a 44pt tap target. Render it ONLY when: `canSend` (not closed), `conversation?.listing` exists and is not deleted, `conversation.listing.status !== 'sold'`, and `conversation.listing.negotiable !== false`. Both roles may use it (a seller opening one is a proactive discount).
  - Reuse the EXISTING `OfferSheet` — import it, do not write a new sheet and do not duplicate the chip logic. Feed it `price`/`currency` from `conversation.listing`.
  - `handleSendOfferInThread(amount)`: build the canonical body `` `${amount}|${currency}|${listedPrice}` `` (same encoding parsed by `MessageBubble` / `ConversationRow`) and call `conversationsAPI.sendMessage(convId, body, 'offer')`. Use the same optimistic-append + rollback + `isNearBottomRef.current = true` pattern as `handleSend`, and `toast.error(t('chat.thread.sendFailed'))` on failure.
  - Extend the renderItem wiring so `onOfferCounter` is also passed for `item.kind === 'offer_counter'` when the message is NOT mine and no `offer_accepted`/`offer_declined` responds to it — i.e. the buyer can counter the seller's counter. `handleOpenCounterSheet` already parses `amount|currency|listedPrice` from `offer.body` and works from either side; only the guard changes.
- `src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx` — surface the Counter action on `offer_counter` bubbles using the existing `onOfferCounter` prop (currently only rendered for `offer`). No new prop names.
- i18n `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json`: `chat.offer.makeOffer`, `chat.offer.counterBack`, plus a11y labels. RTL-safe (composer row already flips via `isRtl`), colors via `useColors()` only.

## API
Existing only: `POST /api/v1/conversations/:id/messages` with `kind: offer` / `offer_counter` and `responds_to_id`, via `conversationsAPI.sendMessage`.

## Out of scope
Do not change message kinds, the backend, the listing-detail offer flow, or the chip percentages. Web mirror (hatiwal-web chat composer) is deliberately NOT part of this card.

## Tests
Jest in `src/screens/chat/__tests__/offerInThread.test.tsx`: button visibility matrix + optimistic send + counter-back guard. Add an `offer_counter` "counterable by buyer" state to `MessageBubble.stories.tsx`.
- **Acceptance**: In an open conversation on a negotiable, non-sold listing, a Tag offer button appears in the composer for both roles; tapping it opens the existing OfferSheet (with its quick-amount chips) prefilled from the pinned listing price; confirming posts a `kind: offer` message with body `amount|currency|listedPrice` via the existing sendMessage endpoint, appears optimistically and rolls back with a toast on failure. The button is hidden when the conversation is closed, when the listing is deleted or sold, and when `listing.negotiable === false` (the existing firm-price notice still shows). An `offer_counter` bubble received from the other participant now shows a Counter action that reopens the counter sheet prefilled with that amount, so a negotiation can run more than one round; my own counters show no Counter button, and a countered/answered offer still suppresses its buttons. No new sheet component and no duplicated chip logic. All 3 locales carry the new keys; works light/dark and RTL (Pashto); Jest suite for the visibility matrix and counter-back guard passes; no console errors.

## TASK-P736
- **Title**: Publish readiness in the listing form: block photoless listings and never let Publish fail silently
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: [MUST FIX — correctness, MED-HIGH] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx:204-217 — the NEW `isEditBlocking` gate can never resolve, turning a soft failure into a dead screen. It reads `isPending`, but the query is `enabled: isEdit && !!listingId`. In @tanstack/react-query 5.101 a DISABLED query keeps `status: 'pending'` with `fetchStatus: 'idle'` (`isLoading = isPending && isFetching`, query-core/src/queryObserver.ts:561), so `isEditPending` is `true` FOREVER whenever `isEdit` is true but `listingId` is falsy. `app/(main)/listing/edit/[id].tsx` feeds `params.id` straight from the URL and `listingId = Number(params.id)`, so `/listing/edit/abc` (deep link, stale notification, bad nav) gives `Number('abc') = NaN` → `!!NaN === false` → query disabled → `isEditBlocking` permanently true → the seller is stuck on `ListingFormSkeleton` with Save AND Publish permanently `disabled`, no error, no retry, no way out but the back button. Before this round the same URL rendered a blank-but-usable form. Note `refetchEditListing()` is also a no-op on a disabled query, so even reaching the EmptyState wouldn't help. FIX: `const isEditBlocking = isEdit && !!listingId && (isEditLoading || (isEditError && !existingListing));` destructuring `isLoading: isEditLoading` instead of `isPending` (false when disabled), and/or render the EmptyState+retry when `isEdit && !listingId`. | [MUST FIX — test coverage, MED] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/seller/__tests__/ — the entire new edit-mode loading/error gate ships with ZERO tests, violating CLAUDE.md's mandatory Jest layer. `grep -rn "listing-form-skeleton|ListingFormSkeleton|common.errorTitle|common.retry" src/screens/seller/__tests__/` returns nothing; `ListingFormSkeleton.tsx` has no test and no story either. This is the fix for the reviewer's own '"never fail silently" became "fails wrongly"' defect — the highest-value behaviour in this round — and nothing pins it. The exact analogous tests already exist to copy from `src/screens/seller/__tests__/MyListingDetail.test.tsx:195-250`. FIX: add to `ListingForm.publish.test.tsx` (a) with `getMyListing` left unresolved: `listing-form-skeleton` visible, pressing `listing.publish` calls no API and fires NO toast (today an un-gated press would toast the wrong blockers); (b) `getMyListing` rejects → `common.errorTitle` + `common.retry` render, pressing retry refetches and the real form appears; (c) `mockParamsState.current = { id: "abc" }` → the screen must not be stuck on the skeleton — this case fails today and pins finding #1. | [MUST FIX — completeness, MED] /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/maestro/browse/full_marketplace_cycle.yaml:22 — P736's publish gate breaks this flow and it was left out of the repair. Line 22 taps `"Publish"` from the ListingForm after only title/price/category — zero photos, no map pin — which is precisely what this card now blocks, so execution can never reach line 24. - **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
`src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx`:
1. `listingSchema` (line ~68) validates title/price/currency/category/lat/long — but **photos are not in the schema at all**, and `onPublish = handleSubmit((values) => publishMutation.mutate(values))` never inspects `photos`. A seller can publish a listing with ZERO photos on a photo-first marketplace; `SellerListingCard.tsx` and `ListingCard` then render the grey "no photo" camera box, which reads as a broken/scam listing to buyers.
2. `handleSubmit` is called with no `onInvalid` handler. The Save/Publish buttons are pinned in the TOP toolbar while the form is a long scroll (photos → title → price → category → condition → description → location → address). Tapping Publish while scrolled to the Address field when e.g. Title or Category is empty produces **no toast, no scroll, no visible change** — the inline error is rendered off-screen. The primary action of the whole seller flow silently does nothing.

## Build (mobile only, no backend change)
- New pure module `src/screens/seller/listing-form/publishReadiness.ts`: `getPublishBlockers({ values, photos })` → ordered `PublishBlocker[]` (`'photos' | 'title' | 'price' | 'category' | 'location'`) in on-screen order, so both the toast copy and the scroll target come from one source of truth. Fully unit-testable, no React.
- `src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx`:
  - Give the `ScrollView` a ref and record each section's `y` via `onLayout` into a `Record<PublishBlocker, number>` ref (wrap the existing `styles.field` views — no layout change).
  - `onPublish = handleSubmit(onValid, onInvalid)`. In BOTH `onInvalid` and a new pre-check inside `onValid` (for `photos.length === 0`, which zod cannot see): show `toast.error(t('listing.form.publishBlocked', { fields: <localized, comma-joined field names> }))` and `scrollTo({ y: firstBlockerY - 12, animated: true })`.
  - Add `photosError` state; pass it to `PhotosSection` so the empty dashed card / strip renders a `colors.destructive` border plus `t('listing.form.photoRequired')`. Clear it as soon as a photo is added.
  - **Save draft stays untouched** — drafts may legitimately have no photos; only Publish (and editing an already-published listing) enforces ≥1 photo.
- `src/screens/seller/listing-form/PhotosSection.tsx`: accept an optional `error?: string` prop and render it; no other behaviour change.
- i18n `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`: `listing.form.publishBlocked`, `listing.form.photoRequired`, and short field names for the toast list. Colors via `useColors()`, RTL-safe text alignment.

## API
No backend change. Existing `POST /api/v1/my/listings`, `PATCH /api/v1/my/listings/:id`, `POST /api/v1/my/listings/:id/publish` via `listingsAPI`.

## Note (not a separate card)
The same silent-invalid-submit exists in the hatiwal-web listing form; mirror it there opportunistically if that file is already open — do NOT create a second board card for it.

## Tests
Jest `src/screens/seller/listing-form/__tests__/publishReadiness.test.ts` (blocker ordering, photo rule, draft exemption) and `src/screens/seller/__tests__/ListingForm.publish.test.tsx` (publish with 0 photos does not call the API and shows the error; publish with a missing off-screen field toasts and scrolls).
- **Acceptance**: Tapping Publish with no photos does NOT call the create/publish API: a localized toast names what is missing, the form scrolls to the Photos section, and PhotosSection shows a destructive border plus a "add at least one photo" message that clears when a photo is added. Tapping Publish with any other invalid field (title, price, category, exact location) while that field is scrolled off-screen always produces a toast listing the missing fields AND scrolls to the first one — Publish is never a silent no-op. Saving as draft is unaffected and still works with zero photos and partial fields. `getPublishBlockers` is a pure exported function with unit tests covering ordering, the photo rule and the draft exemption; a ListingForm test asserts the API is not called. All 3 locales carry the new keys; RTL (Pashto/Dari) alignment correct; no hardcoded colors or strings; no console errors.

## TASK-D428
- **Title**: Chat thread day separators + "unread messages" divider
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-3 (dev DONE with no source changes needed — the aborted cycle-2 agent had already landed the full feature in ce2fe48 via buildThreadRows; Jest and tsc verified green. Both reviews returned): DR BLOCKER groupMessagesByDay.ts:56-65 with Conversation.tsx:268,276-280 — the unread divider MOVES during a live conversation; resolve the boundary message id ONCE and pass it into buildThreadRows | CR HIGH groupMessagesByDay.ts:70 counts messages that MessageBubble.tsx:412-418 never renders — export RESPONSE_KINDS/isRenderableInThread from MessageBubble as the single source of truth | CR MED DaySeparator.tsx:33,35,46 hand-rolls the pill and rules — use RNR Badge + Separator | CR MED no Maestro flow was added or updated (mobile.prompt.md:16) — extend an existing chat flow with assertVisible on the day and unread labels | CR LOW classifyDay goes stale across midnight | CR LOW Conversation.tsx:276-280 buildThreadRows runs on every render — useMemo it and convert capturedUnreadCountRef to state.
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
`src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx` prints only `formatTime(message.createdAt)` on every bubble (lines ~340, 455, 758, 930, 1076, 1136, 1222) — time of day, never a date. `Conversation.tsx` renders one flat `FlatList` of bubbles with no grouping. Consequences:
- A thread spanning days looks like one continuous conversation. A meetup message reading "4:30 PM" gives no clue whether it was agreed today or three weeks ago — directly risky for the meetup flow this app is built around.
- Reopening a thread with unread messages drops the seller at the bottom with no marker for where the new messages start; with 30-message pages they must guess.

## Build (mobile only, no backend change)
- New pure module `src/screens/chat/conversation/groupMessagesByDay.ts`: `buildThreadRows(messages, unreadCount)` → `ThreadRow[]` where a row is `{ type: 'message', message }`, `{ type: 'day', iso }` or `{ type: 'unread' }`. A day row is emitted whenever the calendar day of `createdAt` changes from the previous message; the unread row is inserted before the Nth-from-last INCOMING message when `unreadCount > 0`. Pure, no React, fully unit-tested (empty list, single day, day change, unread larger than loaded page, unread 0).
- New `src/screens/chat/conversation/DaySeparator.tsx` (+ `DaySeparator.stories.tsx`): centered pill using `colors.muted`/`colors.mutedForeground`; label = `t('chat.day.today')` / `t('chat.day.yesterday')` / `formatDate(iso)` from `useLocalization()` so Dari/Pashto get their own date formatting. Second variant renders the unread divider: a hairline rule in `colors.primary` with a centered `t('chat.unreadDivider')` label.
- `src/screens/chat/Conversation.tsx`:
  - Feed the FlatList `buildThreadRows(filteredMessages, initialUnreadCount)` instead of raw `filteredMessages`; `keyExtractor` returns `msg-<id>` / `day-<iso>` / `unread`; `renderItem` branches to `DaySeparator` for the non-message rows and leaves all existing bubble logic (outcome lookup, counter suppression, delete) untouched — those lookups must keep using the full `messages` array, not the row list.
  - Capture the unread boundary ONCE per load: in the `useFocusEffect`, `await load(id)` BEFORE calling `markRead(id)` (they currently race), and store `conversation.unreadCount` from that first payload in a ref. The divider must not reappear on subsequent silent refreshes of the same open thread.
  - While chat search is active, day separators are recomputed from the filtered set and the unread divider is suppressed (search results are not a timeline).
- i18n `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json`: `chat.day.today`, `chat.day.yesterday`, `chat.unreadDivider`.

## API
None. Uses `createdAt` already on every message and `unreadCount` already on the `Conversation` payload from `GET /api/v1/conversations/:id`.

## Out of scope
No change to bubble internals, no sticky/floating date header, no backend, no read-receipt changes.
- **Acceptance**: A thread whose messages span more than one calendar day shows a centered day separator above the first message of each day, labelled "Today"/"Yesterday" for the two most recent days and a locale-formatted date (via useLocalization) otherwise. Opening a conversation that had unread messages shows a single "Unread messages" divider immediately above the first unread incoming message; it does not reappear after the thread silently refreshes while still open, and it never appears when unreadCount is 0. The conversation is loaded before mark-read is called, so the boundary is captured pre-read. With chat search active, separators reflect the filtered results and the unread divider is hidden. All existing bubble behaviour (meetup/offer outcome lookup, counter suppression, delete, read receipts, load-older pagination, scroll-to-bottom on send) is unchanged. `buildThreadRows` is a pure exported function with unit tests for empty list, single day, day change, unread > loaded page and unread 0; DaySeparator has a Storybook entry for both variants; all 3 locales present; works light/dark and RTL (Pashto); no console errors.

## TASK-WEB-D2READ
- **Title**: Web chat: messages have no time, no day separators and no sent/seen ticks — add the bubble meta row (D2-READ)
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-4: dev DONE — finished the on-disk partial work, verified all 10 acceptance items, ran chat-receipts 8 plus conversation-thread 4 = 12/12 green, and added the missing offer_counter coverage. The work is committed in 56ec7b0. BOTH REVIEWS ARE OWED — both reviewers were spawned and both errored on the session limit. Re-review only; do NOT rebuild.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code, not from the catalog)
`hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/message-bubble.tsx` renders body, offer cards, meetup cards and attachments — and **never renders `createdAt` or `readAt`**. `grep -rn "createdAt" hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/*.tsx` returns NOTHING, and `readAt` appears exactly once in the whole web client (`src/lib/types.ts:194`, declared but unused). So on web a buyer cannot tell whether a message was sent 3 minutes or 3 weeks ago, and — the trust-critical half — cannot tell whether the seller has SEEN their message. Mobile ships both: `hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/MessageBubble.tsx` prints the time on every bubble plus a single `Check` (sent) / overlapping double `Check` (`ReadReceipt`, line 161) when `message.readAt` is set. The catalog marks D2-READ as ❓ verify-first; it is genuinely unbuilt.

The data is already on the wire: `hatiwal-api/app/serializers/message_serializer.rb:2` serializes `:read_at, :created_at`, and `PUT /conversations/:id/mark_read` (`conversations_controller.rb:64-71`) stamps `read_at` on the other party's messages. **No API or contract change.**

## Build
1. EDIT `hatiwal-web/src/lib/format.ts` — add `formatTime(iso, locale)` using the existing `INTL_TAG` map (`Intl.DateTimeFormat(..., { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })`), returning `""` for a missing/invalid date exactly like `formatDate`. Formatting stays in this one module (house rule: `format.ts` is the only place dates are formatted).
2. ADD `hatiwal-web/src/lib/message-days.ts` — one exported `dayKey(iso: string): string` returning the LOCAL calendar day (`YYYY-MM-DD`, not UTC slice) so the separator logic has a single implementation shared by the component and its test.
3. EDIT `src/components/chat/message-bubble.tsx` — add a meta row as the last child INSIDE the bubble: `formatTime(m.createdAt, locale)` in `text-[11px]` reusing the existing `mutedMeta` class (so it stays legible on both the primary `mine` bubble and the muted incoming one), and for `mine` messages a tick after the time — `Check` (size-3) when `m.readAt == null`, `CheckCheck` when set, with `title`/`aria-label` from new keys `chat.message.sent` / `chat.message.seen`. The row must render for text, attachment/image, `offer`, `offer_counter` and `meetup_proposal` bubbles (they all share the one wrapper). It must NOT render on the centred `system` pill, the accepted/declined pills, or the deleted tombstone — mirroring mobile.
4. EDIT `src/components/chat/conversation-thread.tsx` — render a day separator (centred `rounded-full bg-muted px-3 py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground` chip, same treatment as the existing system pill) before the first message of each `dayKey` while mapping `visibleMessages`: label = `chat.day.today` for today, `chat.day.yesterday` for yesterday, otherwise `formatDate(m.createdAt, locale)`. Separators are suppressed while an in-thread search query is active (filtered results are not contiguous days). Do not touch the existing seed effect (`:108-110`) or the cache-sync effect (`:117-119`) beyond what is needed — a refetch that brings back a flipped `readAt` must not drop messages sent during the visit.
5. EDIT `hatiwal-web/messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json` — add `chat.message.sent`, `chat.message.seen`, `chat.day.today`, `chat.day.yesterday` in all 3 locales (nothing hardcoded).

Out of scope: broadcasting a read-state change over ActionCable (Rails only broadcasts new messages). The tick flips on the sender's next fetch of the thread — that is the documented behaviour, do not add polling.

## Tests
ADD `hatiwal-web/e2e/chat-receipts.spec.ts`. The mock fixture already supports it: `e2e/mock-api/server.mjs:148-158` has message id 2 (mine, `read_at` set), id 3 (incoming, `read_at: null`) and id 1 created a day earlier (2026-06-20 vs 06-21) — add one `mine` message with `read_at: null` for the sent-not-seen case.
- **Acceptance**: 1) `/en/conversations/1` shows a time on every text, offer, counter, meetup and attachment bubble; the centred system/accepted/declined pills and the deleted tombstone show none. 2) A message I sent that the other party has read shows a double tick; one they have not read shows a single tick; incoming messages show no tick at all. 3) The tick has an accessible name ("Sent"/"Seen" in en) from the new keys — verified with a Playwright `getByTitle`/`aria-label` assertion, not a screenshot. 4) A thread spanning two days renders exactly one day separator between them, labelled "Today"/"Yesterday" when applicable and an absolute date otherwise; a single-day thread renders none. 5) Opening the in-thread search hides the separators and the existing match-count/highlight behaviour is unchanged. 6) After the recipient opens the thread, returning to (or reloading) the sender's thread flips the tick to double without losing any message sent earlier in that visit. 7) Sending a new message immediately shows its time plus a single tick, and the composer/draft behaviour is unchanged. 8) All 4 new keys exist in en/ps/fa; verified on `/ps` and `/fa` — time + tick sit on the correct (mirrored) side of the bubble, digits are not clipped — in light and dark. 9) `e2e/chat-receipts.spec.ts` passes and the existing `conversation-thread.spec.ts` still passes. 10) `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

## TASK-WEB-R612
- **Title**: Web: after reporting a user, offer to block them (Report → Block follow-up; block endpoint already wired)
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-3 (dev DONE — the killed cycle-2 agent had already landed this in 9713ba8, so it was audited against mobile, proven with E2E and cleaned up rather than rebuilt. DESIGN REVIEW IS STILL OWED — it died in the session limit). Code review returned: HIGH conversation-thread.tsx:435 alreadyBlocked={blocked} is fed the wrong meaning — either drop the prop (mobile's ReportSheet has none) or make it mean "the current user has blocked them" | HIGH report-button.tsx:117-131 confirmBlock calls blockUser() raw, violating the React Query rule — convert to useMutation and invalidate ["blocked-users"], ["conversations"], ["conversation"] | MED sellers/[id]/page.tsx:94 behaves differently across the three surfaces — read the ["blocked-users"] query inside report-button.tsx instead | MED report-button.tsx:110-112 the error path discards the API body, leaving 9 dead translated keys — attach the parsed errors array in lib/api/me.ts:31-36.
- **Description**: ## The gap (verified in code)
`hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/report-button.tsx:66-88` ends a user report with `toast.success(t('report.success'))` and closes the dialog. The reporter — who just said "this person is abusive/fraudulent" — is given no way to stop that person contacting them, even though `blockUser()` already exists on web (`src/lib/api/chat.ts:150`, `POST /users/:id/block`, already allow-listed at `src/app/api/me/[...path]/route.ts:48`) and `/settings/blocked-users` already manages the result. Mobile closed this loop in TASK-R612 (DONE); `report.block` is `null` in `hatiwal-web/messages/en.json`, confirming web never did. Catalog row R612 is ⬜. This is a 3-surface trust win in one component: listing detail, `/sellers/[id]`, and the `/conversations/[id]` header all render `ReportButton`.

## Build (client-only; no API, no backend, no new endpoint)
1. EDIT `src/components/shared/report-button.tsx`: on a successful `createReport` where `reportableType === "User"`, keep the existing success toast, close the report dialog, then open a follow-up `ConfirmDialog` (`@/components/ui/confirm-dialog` — the house confirm, never a bare `window.confirm`) with title `report.block.title`, description `report.block.body`, confirm `report.block.confirmCta`, cancel `report.block.cancel`, `destructive`. Confirm → `await blockUser(reportableId)` then `toast.success(t('report.block.success'))`; failure → `toast.error(t('report.block.error'))` and the report still stands (never roll back a report). Cancel is a pure no-op. A `Listing` report keeps today's behaviour exactly (no prompt).
2. Two new optional props, mirroring the fix mobile needed: `alreadyBlocked?: boolean` (when true, skip the prompt entirely — never offer to block someone already blocked) and `onBlocked?: () => void` (called only after a successful block).
3. EDIT `src/components/chat/conversation-thread.tsx:397-403` — the header `ReportButton` passes `alreadyBlocked={blocked}` and `onBlocked={() => setBlocked(true)}` so the shield icon in the same header flips to "unblock" without a reload (the thread already owns that state). `/sellers/[id]` and `/listings/[id]` pass neither (server components with no local block state) and must keep working unchanged.
4. EDIT `messages/en.json`, `ps.json`, `fa.json` — add `report.block.{title,body,confirmCta,cancel,success,error}`. Copy the wording verbatim from `hatiwal-mobile/src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/report.json` (`block` block already exists there in all 3 locales) so the two clients read identically.

## Tests
Extend `hatiwal-web/e2e/report.spec.ts`: the mock server already handles `POST /users/:id/block` (`e2e/mock-api/server.mjs:491-492`), so assert the request is issued on confirm and not issued on cancel.
- **Acceptance**: 1) Reporting a user from `/sellers/[id]` shows the report success toast, then a confirm dialog titled "Block this user?" offering "Yes, block them" / "Not now". 2) Confirming issues exactly one `POST /users/:id/block` with the reported user's id and shows the block success toast; the user then appears on `/settings/blocked-users`. 3) "Not now" closes with nothing blocked and no second request. 4) A block failure shows the error toast and the report is still recorded (it appears on `/settings/reports`). 5) Reporting a LISTING shows only the existing success toast — no block prompt — on `/listings/[id]`. 6) In the `/conversations/[id]` header, reporting the participant and confirming flips the header shield to the unblock state with no reload; opening report on an already-blocked participant shows NO block prompt. 7) All 6 new keys present in en/ps/fa with the same wording as mobile; dialog verified on `/ps` and `/fa` (RTL button order, no clipping) in light and dark. 8) Playwright specs cover confirm-blocks, cancel-does-not-block, and listing-report-shows-no-prompt; existing `report.spec.ts` cases still pass. 9) `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.

## TASK-V395
- **Title**: "Save draft" silently does nothing and demands a map pin — make drafts genuinely saveable and never silent
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: BUILT IN CYCLE 3, BOTH REVIEWS OWED (they died on the session limit) — re-review only, do NOT rebuild. Dev report: Save draft genuinely saves now — draft mode exempts location and photos in publishReadiness.ts, lat/lng are optional in the zod schema, a new locationError state keeps Publish fully gated, and onInvalid gives a toast plus scroll. Committed in 8927dd4. Reviewers should pay particular attention to acceptance #3 and #4 — that Publish and the edit-a-published-listing path are still fully gated.
- **Description**: In src/screens/seller/ListingForm.tsx the toolbar "Save draft" button was handleSubmit((values) => saveMutation.mutate(values)) with NO onInvalid handler, and the single zod resolver required latitude/longitude via z.coerce.number().finite() for EVERY submit path. Two user-visible consequences: (a) a seller with photos and a title but no map pin taps Save draft and NOTHING happens — no toast, no scroll, no spinner, the only feedback being an inline error far below the fold, so the button looks broken and the half-written listing feels unsaveable; (b) you cannot save a draft until you have dropped an exact map pin, even though hatiwal-api's app/models/listing.rb validates only title, price > 0, currency and category (lines 21-25) — the server happily stores a pin-less draft, only the client forbids it, and drafts are by definition incomplete. BUILD (mobile only, NO backend change, NO new endpoint): extend publishReadiness.ts mode "draft" so it exempts location as well as photos, and update the header comment to state the new contract (a draft needs title + price + category, mirroring the Rails validations; publish additionally needs at least one photo and coordinates), keeping PUBLISH_BLOCKER_ORDER and the function pure and React-free. Make the zod resolver the DRAFT schema — latitude/longitude become .optional() while title/price/currency/category stay required — which is safe because onPublish and onSavePublished ALREADY recompute the full blocker list themselves via getPublishBlockers({values, photos, mode:"publish"}) before mutating. Because zod no longer sets errors.latitude, add a locationError state mirroring the existing photosError, set inside handlePublishBlockers when blockers.includes("location") and cleared in the LocationRangePicker onConfirm, and drive the location row's destructive border/icon/message from locationError ?? errors.latitude so the Publish red-state UX is preserved. Give onSaveDraft an onInvalid that calls handlePublishBlockers(getPublishBlockers({values: getValues(), photos, mode:"draft"})) plus a draft-mode pre-check in the valid path, so a blocked draft always produces a toast naming the missing fields AND scrolls to the first one, exactly like Publish. i18n: add form.draftBlocked ("Add {{fields}} to save this draft") in en/ps/fa reusing the existing BLOCKER_LABEL_KEY field names; form.publishBlocked stays as-is. Out of scope: the AsyncStorage autosave/restore banner, PhotosSection, the duplicate/relist flow, and anything on the publish path beyond the location error now coming from state instead of zod.
- **Acceptance**: 1) With a title, price and category filled in but NO map pin, tapping Save draft creates the draft via POST /my/listings and routes to the new listing's owner detail (today it does nothing at all). 2) With the title empty, Save draft shows a toast naming exactly the missing fields ("Add Title, Price to save this draft", localized) and scrolls to the first one — never silent — and photos/location are NOT listed as draft blockers. 3) Publish is unchanged and still fully gated: no photos or no map pin cannot be published, the publish-blocked toast lists the missing fields including Photos and Location, scrolls to the first, and the location row shows its destructive border/message driven by the new locationError state, cleared immediately when a pin is added. 4) Editing an already-published listing still enforces the full publish rules on its single Save button, so a live listing can never be left with zero photos or no coordinates. 5) A pin-less draft survives a round trip — reopening it shows "Tap to set location", and publishing from there still requires the pin. 6) getPublishBlockers stays a pure, unit-tested function and remains the single source of both the toast copy and the scroll target for draft AND publish, with no duplicated blocker logic in the screen. 7) form.draftBlocked exists in en/ps/fa, verified in Pashto in light and dark, colors from useColors() only, no raw Alert. 8) publishReadiness unit tests cover draft-vs-publish for missing photos, missing location, missing title/price/category and the fully-valid case; ListingForm.draft.test.tsx plus the existing publish/duplicate suites pass; maestro/seller/save_draft.yaml green; no backend change and no console errors.

MyListingDetail.tsx: render SaleBuyerCard immediately below the status/expiry row, before PriceTag, inside the existing animated Section. my-listings/SellerListingCard.tsx: one compact localized line under the ExpiryBadge, text only, no second avatar stack. i18n listing.json en/ps/fa: sale.reservedFor, sale.soldTo, sale.messageBuyer, sale.finalPrice, sale.noBuyerRecorded. Colors via useColors() only, row direction via isRtl, new maestro/seller/reserved_buyer.yaml. WEB MIRROR is a follow-up card, not this one — do not touch hatiwal-web here.
- **Acceptance**: 1) GET /api/v1/my/listings/:id on a listing reserved via the buyer picker returns a sale object with the buyer's id/name/avatar_url/verified, final_price + currency, status, and the conversation_id of that buyer's conversation on this listing; a reserve/sold done WITHOUT a buyer returns sale: null. 2) The public GET /api/v1/listings/:id payload contains NO sale key for a guest, for a random buyer, and for the owner — the privacy assertion is an actual rspec example, not a comment. 3) GET /api/v1/my/listings includes sale per row and issues no per-row buyer/transaction query (eager-loaded). 4) The reserve and sold endpoints still return {listing:, transaction:} with unchanged keys — no client contract break for existing mobile/web callers. 5) On mobile, a reserved listing's owner detail shows "Reserved for Ahmad" with Ahmad's avatar and verified tag via the shared UserIdentity, the agreed final price when it differs from the asking price, and a "Message Ahmad" button landing directly in that existing conversation thread. 6) A sold listing shows "Sold to {name}"; a draft/active listing and a legacy buyer-less reserve show no card at all — no empty box, no placeholder avatar. 7) My Listings cards show the compact "Reserved for {name}" line for reserved/sold rows only. 8) All 5 keys exist in en/ps/fa, verified in Pashto (row mirrors correctly, name not clipped) in both light and dark, colors only from useColors(). 9) Jest tests for SaleBuyerCard (reserved / sold / null sale / null conversationId fallback) and a Storybook entry for each state; maestro/seller/reserved_buyer.yaml green. 10) bundle exec rspec and bundle exec rubocop clean; no console errors.

Its cases (:201-390) are the ONLY coverage of acceptance #4 (a chip tap actually issues `conversationsAPI.getConversations({ role })` from the screen), #6 (the trap the ticket flagged as must-test: while a role filter is active the fetcher must not overwrite the chat-tab unread badge with the role-scoped subset - `if (query.page === 1 && !roleParam)`) and #7 (the role-aware empty states as rendered); src/api/__tests__/conversations.test.ts:125-175 only proves the fetcher appends the param, and the badge-trap assertion exists nowhere else.
