FIX: add minHeight: 44 and paddingVertical: 11 to both groups - they share one row so they must match - or route both through one shared chip. | MUST ConversationRow.tsx:200-212 renders `Badge variant="muted"` for both roles - identical grey, text only, no icon - so Buying vs Selling is an 11px text read and the Pashto labels differ by two glyphs; and because renderItem (Conversations.tsx:330-339) passes only item + tabMode and never `role`, once Selling is active every row repeats "Selling", eating width in titleGroup so the listing title (styles.title flexShrink: 1) truncates earlier for zero information gain. FIX: give the pill the same lucide Store / ShoppingBag icons the chips use (Conversations.tsx:604-616) so the roles separate without relying on colour, and pass `role` into ConversationRow so the pill is skipped while a role scope is active. | MUST light-mode contrast on the role pill: badge.tsx renders variant "muted" as mutedForeground on muted and hardcodes fontSize 11 / fontWeight 700 (the `style` prop reaches only the View, not the Text); with useColors.ts:43-44 light values that is ~4.29:1, under the 4.5:1 AA floor for normal text (dark is fine at ~5.8:1). FIX: use variant="secondary" for the role pill, or let Badge take a text-colour override and pass colors.foreground. | MED the composition path the flow claims to cover is untested: the trailing block of maestro/chat/conversations_role_filter.yaml is commented "Role filter composes with the Archived tab too", but both chips were deselected two steps earlier so `role === null` and it exercises a bare Inbox<->Archived switch. FIX: select a role chip, THEN switch to Archived and assert the archived list is still role-scoped (or assert the documented reset once the invisible-filter item is resolved); that variant would also have surfaced the invisible-filter bug. | MED Conversations.tsx:596-618 - the new role chips carry no accessibilityRole="button", no accessibilityState={{ selected }} and no accessibilityLabel, so a screen-reader user cannot tell which scope is active (ConditionChips.tsx:51-53 and CategoryChipRow.tsx:147-148, :193-194 all set them); they are also bare Pressables with no android_ripple, no haptic and no active-state animation, whereas CategoryChipRow uses AnimatedPressable with haptic and a reduce-motion-aware spring, so the only feedback for a chip that triggers a server round-trip is the list eventually changing. FIX: add the a11y props and match the AnimatedPressable + haptic treatment. | MED the role chips are a 4th inline copy of the chip pattern three lines above them (the read-state chips), already extracted twice in src/components/common (ConditionChips.tsx, CategoryChipRow.tsx, plus ListingFiltersBar.tsx) - see docs/REFACTOR_DUPLICATION.md. FIX: extract one `FilterChip` into src/components/common (label, icon, active, onPress, a11y, minHeight 44) and have both groups consume it, which also closes the touch-target and a11y items in one place. | MED two competing active treatments in one row: read-state chips fill with colors.secondary when active (Conversations.tsx:554) while role chips fill with colors.primary (:610), which is also the Inbox/Archived tab's active fill (:494), so a selected role chip carries the same weight as the top-level tab one divider away. FIX: use one active token for all chips (secondary) and reserve primary for the tab toggle; if the groups need separating use a small leading group label, not a heavier fill. | MED no scroll affordance on a row guaranteed to overflow: five chips plus divider and gaps are ~400px of content in ~343px of usable width at 375px (acceptance #3's own premise), yet showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} (Conversations.tsx:530) with no edge fade and no peek, so Buying/Selling can sit entirely off-screen on first open and never be discovered. FIX: add a trailing-edge gradient fade or tighten chip padding so all five fit. | MED RTL never verified, though acceptance #8 and DESIGN_SYSTEM.md:159 make Pashto mirroring a gate: the code does follow `isRtl ? "row-reverse" : "row"`, but the app calls I18nManager.forceRTL (src/i18n/index.ts:55, :64, :83), so the native horizontal ScrollView already starts at the trailing edge while the content container is additionally row-reverse - in an overflowing row that decides whether "All" or "Selling" is the first chip visible and it will not match LTR. FIX: one Pashto screenshot in light and dark before Done, plus the same check on ConversationRow's title + role pill + timestamp row with a long title. | LOW testID `role-pill-{id}` (ConversationRow.tsx:202) is never referenced by the new flow even though the per-row role hint is half the card's title and acceptance #5 covers it. FIX: assert the pill in the mixed inbox and add one long-title row confirming the timestamp survives. | LOW maestro/_helpers/login.yaml (inherited) defaults to `buyer@hatiwal.test` / `Password123!`, but the seeded dev users are `<name>@hatiwal.com` / `password123` and the `.test` credentials 401, so the new flow cannot pass locally without EMAIL/PASSWORD overrides - corroborating it was only YAML-parsed, never run. FIX: fix the shared fixture so the chat flows are runnable. HISTORY: the backend side was reviewed clean (policy_scope + paginate_blue, no `render json:`, frozen ROLES constant, role filter no-ops on unknown values, no injection surface, participant-based policy, existing buyer_id/seller_id indexes, no new N+1), as were the mobile lint-level rules (copy via t(...), colours via useColors(), no raw Alert.alert, useFocusEffect present, row-reverse on chip/tab/title rows, no function-style Pressable styles, all chat.filter.*/chat.role.*/chat.empty.* keys in en/ps/fa).
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
One Hatiwal account is both buyer and seller, but the chat inbox gives zero signal about which side you are on in a thread. `app/serializers/conversation_serializer.rb` `:list` view sends only `other_participant` — no buyer/seller id, no viewer role — so `src/screens/chat/conversations/ConversationRow.tsx` literally cannot render a hint, and it doesn't. `GET /conversations` supports `archived` and `listing_id` only (see `conversations_controller.rb#index` and `src/api/conversations.ts` `getConversations`), so there is no way to see just "people asking about my items" vs "sellers I'm asking about theirs". A seller with 20 buyer threads and their own buying threads mixed in cannot triage at all.

## Backend (hatiwal-api)
- `app/models/conversation.rb`: add `scope :as_buyer_for, ->(user) { where(buyer_id: user.id) }` and `scope :as_seller_for, ->(user) { where(seller_id: user.id) }` next to the existing `for_user` / `archived_for` scopes.
- `app/controllers/api/v1/conversations_controller.rb#index`: accept a `role` param and apply it to `base_scope` before `policy_scope`. Allowed values in a frozen constant (`ROLES = { buying: "buying", selling: "selling" }.freeze` — no bare literals in the branch, per the house rule); anything absent or unrecognised keeps today's behaviour (all conversations). Must compose with `archived`, `not_deleted_for`, `listing_id`, ordering, the existing eager-loads and the single-query `unread_counts` GROUP BY (no new N+1).
- `app/serializers/conversation_serializer.rb`: add `field(:viewer_role)` to BOTH `:list` and `:detailed` — `"buyer"` when `c.buyer_id == opts[:current_user]&.id`, `"seller"` otherwise, `nil` when there is no `current_user`. Uses ids already loaded, so no extra query.
- RSpec/RSwag in `spec/requests/api/v1/conversations_spec.rb`: `role=selling` returns only threads where the caller is the seller; `role=buying` only buyer threads; a bogus role returns everything (no 500, no empty list); `role` composes with `archived=true`; `viewer_role` is correct for both participants of the same conversation. `bundle exec rspec` green + `bundle exec rubocop` no offenses.

## Mobile
- `src/api/conversations.ts`: add `viewerRole?: "buyer" | "seller"` to the `Conversation` interface; add `role?: "buying" | "selling"` to `getConversations` params → `query.append("role", role)`.
- `src/screens/chat/Conversations.tsx`: do NOT add a fourth header row (the header already carries title+badge, SearchBar, Inbox/Archived tabs and the All/Unread/Read control — it is already ~200px). Replace the 3-segment All/Unread/Read block with ONE horizontally-scrollable chip row holding two independent groups: read-state (All | Unread | Read — all three preserved, still client-side exactly as today) and role (Buying | Selling, mutually exclusive, deselectable back to "both"). Role goes into the fetcher (server-side, so it stays correct across infinite-scroll pages) and into `listConfig.id` so switching re-fetches; role also applies in the Archived tab.
- TRAP to handle explicitly: the unread tab badge is derived from page 1 of `allConversationsRef`. While a role filter is active that page holds only one role, so the fetcher must NOT overwrite `setUnreadMessageTotal` / `setUnreadBadgeCount` when `role` is set — keep the last unfiltered total. Cover this with a test.
- `ConversationRow.tsx`: render a compact muted role pill beside the listing title using the shared RNR `Badge` (`t("chat.role.selling")` / `t("chat.role.buying")`), only when `item.viewerRole` is present; the title keeps `flexShrink: 1` so the timestamp never gets pushed out. No new avatar/identity UI (UserIdentity stays as-is).
- Role-aware empty states via the existing `emptyIcon`/`emptyTitle`/`emptyDescription`/`emptyAction` switch: Selling → "No one has messaged you about your items yet" + CTA Post a listing (`/(main)/listing/new`); Buying → "You haven't messaged any seller yet" + CTA Browse.
- Translations in `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json`: `chat.role.buying/selling`, `chat.filter.buying/selling`, `chat.empty.sellingTitle/sellingDescription/buyingTitle/buyingDescription`. All colors via `useColors()`; chip row RTL-safe (row-reverse + scrolls correctly in Pashto).
- Tests: extend `src/screens/chat/conversations/__tests__/ConversationRow.test.tsx` (pill for buyer, for seller, absent when `viewerRole` undefined); new Conversations screen test asserting the Selling chip calls `conversationsAPI.getConversations` with `role: "selling"` and that the unread badge is not overwritten while a role filter is active; add buying/selling variants to `ConversationRow.stories.tsx`; Maestro flow `maestro/chat/inbox_role_filter.yaml` (happy path + empty Selling state).

## Out of scope
No change to archive/mark-read/delete behaviour, the thread screen, ActionCable, or the chat tab badge wiring. The web mirror (`hatiwal-web/src/components/chat/conversations-view.tsx`) is a separate `type:web` card to be proposed once this `role` / `viewer_role` contract ships — do not build it here.
- **Acceptance**: 1) `GET /conversations?role=selling` returns only threads where the caller is the seller, `role=buying` only buyer threads, an unknown/absent role returns all — and each composes with `archived=true` and `listing_id`. 2) Every conversation in both `:list` and `:detailed` carries `viewer_role`, correct for each side of the same thread. 3) The inbox header still has exactly three control rows; the new chip row shows All/Unread/Read plus Buying/Selling and scrolls without clipping at 375px width. 4) Tapping Selling refetches from the server (verified: the request carries role=selling) and the list shows only threads where I'm the seller; tapping it again returns to both. 5) Each row shows a Buying or Selling pill next to the listing title, and the timestamp is still fully visible with a long title. 6) With Selling active, the chat tab unread badge keeps its unfiltered total (does not drop to the role's subset). 7) Empty Selling shows the "no one has messaged you about your items" state with a working Post-a-listing CTA; empty Buying shows the Browse CTA. 8) 3 locales complete; Pashto RTL mirrors the chip row and the pill placement; light and dark both readable (no hardcoded colors). 9) `bundle exec rspec` and `bundle exec rubocop` green on the API; `npm test` green on mobile; new Jest tests + Storybook variants + the Maestro flow all present and passing.

## TASK-L863
- **Title**: Seller listing actions: one primary + a "More" sheet, and one shared lifecycle hook (kills the 6-button card and the conflicting primaries)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: CYCLE-4: dev DONE and committed in bed7a2d — built src/hooks/useListingLifecycle.ts and src/components/common/ListingActionsSheet.tsx, then rewired SellerListingCard.tsx and MyListingDetail.tsx onto them, killing ~200 duplicated lines of seven copy-pasted mutations and the 6-button card, and resolving the two screens disagreeing about the primary action for the same status. BOTH REVIEWS ARE OWED — both reviewers were spawned and both errored on the session limit. Re-review only; do NOT rebuild. Reviewers should check acceptance #3 hardest: that EVERY action previously reachable still is, including Mark sold on an expired-active listing and Duplicate on a sold listing.
- **Description**: ## Problem (verified in code)
Every card in My Shop renders up to six buttons. For an `active` listing `src/screens/seller/my-listings/SellerListingCard.tsx` shows primary **Mark sold** plus outline **Mark reserved / Unpublish / Edit / Duplicate / Delete**, which wraps onto two-to-three rows and adds well over 100px to each card — on a 6.1" phone barely one listing fits above the fold, and the "what's my next step?" rule from BACKLOG C2 is drowned. C2 explicitly specs `any→Edit/Delete (overflow)`; the overflow was never built.
Worse, the surfaces disagree about the SAME status: `SellerListingCard.tsx:299` makes **Mark sold** the primary for `active`, `MyListingDetail.tsx:393` makes **Mark reserved** the primary for `active`. The cause is duplication — the seven `useMutation`s (publish/unpublish/activate/reserve/markSold/renew/delete), the six `confirmAlert` handlers, the invalidation sets and the BuyerPicker/ReviewPrompt wiring are copy-pasted in both files (~200 duplicated lines) and drifted apart.

## Build
1. NEW `src/hooks/useListingLifecycle.ts` — the single source of truth. `useListingLifecycle({ listing, onDone })` returns `{ primaryAction, moreActions, isBusy, buyerPicker, reviewPrompt }` and owns: all seven mutations (`listingsAPI.publishListing/unpublishListing/activateListing/reserveListing/markSold/renewListing/deleteListing`), the per-action `confirmAlert` copy (never raw `Alert.alert`), the `sonner-native` toasts, invalidation of `["my-listings"]`, `["myListingStatusCounts"]`, `["my-listing", id]` and `["conversations", listing.id]`, and the BuyerPickerSheet + ReviewPromptSheet state (reserve/sold open the picker; a sold response carrying `transaction.buyer` opens the review prompt). Canonical primary: `draft`→Publish, `active`→Mark reserved, `reserved`→Mark sold, `sold`→none, `active && expired`→Renew. Everything else lands in `moreActions`. No behaviour and no variant may be dropped — expired-active must still offer Mark sold, reserved must still offer Activate, and Duplicate must stay available for every status.
2. NEW `src/components/common/ListingActionsSheet.tsx` + `.stories.tsx` — labeled action rows with lucide icons, destructive Delete last behind a separator, safe-area bottom padding, `useColors()`, RTL row-reverse. Build it the same way as the existing sheets (`src/screens/chat/conversation/ComposerActionsSheet.tsx` from TASK-K487 and `src/components/common/BuyerPickerSheet.tsx`) — do not invent a new sheet mechanism and do not hand-roll rows a shared component already provides.
3. EDIT `src/screens/seller/my-listings/SellerListingCard.tsx` — the actions block becomes exactly two controls: the full-width primary `Button` plus a compact outline "More" button (`MoreHorizontal`) that opens `ListingActionsSheet`. Delete the local mutations/handlers and consume the hook.
4. EDIT `src/screens/seller/MyListingDetail.tsx` — section 7 gets the identical two-control pattern (primary + More), local mutations/handlers deleted, Edit/Duplicate/Delete keep their icons as sheet rows.
5. Translations: only one new key, `listing.actions.more`, in `src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/listing.json`; every action label reuses the existing keys.

## Out of scope
Do NOT touch `src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingHeader.tsx`, `src/screens/chat/conversation/reserveAfterAccept.ts`, the buyer-side `ListingDetail.tsx`, the status tabs/counts (TASK-M826), or any endpoint — this card changes zero API calls.

## Tests
Update `src/screens/seller/my-listings/__tests__/SellerListingCard.test.tsx` so actions are asserted through the sheet (open More, then tap). New `src/hooks/__tests__/useListingLifecycle.test.tsx`: per-status primary/more mapping incl. the expired case, `confirmAlert` fires before every mutation, destructive styling on Delete, and the exact invalidation keys. `ListingActionsSheet.stories.tsx` in light/dark/RTL. Maestro `maestro/seller/listing_actions_sheet.yaml` covering publish-from-draft and delete-from-sheet.
- **Acceptance**: 1) An active listing card shows exactly two controls — "Mark reserved" and "More" — and at least two full cards fit above the fold on a 6.1" screen where only one did before. 2) The primary action for a given status is now identical on the card and on MyListingDetail (draft→Publish, active→Mark reserved, reserved→Mark sold, expired→Renew, sold→none). 3) Every action available before is still reachable from the More sheet: Mark sold, Mark reserved, Unpublish, Activate, Renew, Edit, Duplicate, Delete — including Mark sold on an expired-active listing and Duplicate on a sold listing. 4) Delete still goes through `confirmAlert` with destructive styling and a success toast; no raw `Alert.alert` anywhere in the touched files. 5) Reserve and Mark sold still open BuyerPickerSheet, and a sold sale with a recorded buyer still opens ReviewPromptSheet. 6) After any action the list, the status-count pills and the owner detail all refresh with no manual pull-to-refresh (invalidation keys asserted in the hook test). 7) `SellerListingCard.tsx` and `MyListingDetail.tsx` contain no `useMutation` for lifecycle actions — all of it lives in `useListingLifecycle.ts`. 8) `npm test` exits 0 with the updated SellerListingCard suite plus the new hook tests; Storybook shows the sheet in light, dark and RTL. 9) Pashto RTL: sheet rows mirror, icons sit on the correct side, nothing clips. 10) 3 locales complete (only `listing.actions.more` is new).

- **Title**: Chat thread dead end — explicit "item reserved/sold" state with a reason for the vanished offer button and a real next step

- **ReviewNotes**: MUST FIX (MEDIUM — accessibility rule violation, previously-fixed regression re-introduced) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingUnavailableNotice.tsx:333-341. ISSUE: the "View their listings" recovery CTA was demoted to `variant="ghost" size="sm"` with `style={{ alignSelf: "center" }}` and no hitSlop. `size="sm"` resolves to `{ paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 6, minHeight: 36 }` (src/components/reusables/button.tsx:71), and `alignSelf: "center"` also shrinks the hit area horizontally to the text width — so the target went from a full-width 44pt button to a ~36pt content-width text link. docs/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md:80 is explicit: "**Touch targets**: ≥ 44px. Icon buttons `h-9 w-9` minimum with `hitSlop`", and docs/TESTING.md:575 gates it in the story checklist. This is the SAME defect a prior review already caught and fixed in this repo: docs/SPRINT_BOARD_ARCHIVE.md:293 — "Both Edit buttons ... used size='sm' (which sets minHeight:36 ...) — below the 44pt accessibility minimum. Added minHeight:44 to both Button style overrides" (the fix is still live at src/screens/shared/Profile.tsx:238-246, a ghost+sm button with `minHeight: 44`). Note the reviewer's option (b) asked for a "single text-link row", not for a sub-44pt target. FAILURE SCENARIO: a ps/fa buyer on a mid-range Android device taps the only remaining seller-recovery action on a reserved/sold thread and misses it — the visible text is ~14px tall inside a 36pt box with no hitSlop and no border, and it is the LAST item above the composer, i.e. in the thumb zone where mis-taps land on the message input. FIX: keep the visual demotion but restore the effective target — `hitSlop={{ top: 6, bottom: 6, left: 16, right: 16 }}` on the Button (ButtonProps extends TouchableOpacityProps and `{...props}` is spread onto TouchableOpacity, so it passes through; this is the pattern DESIGN_SYSTEM.md:80 itself sanctions and it preserves the ~12px vertical reclaim) — or, if you prefer the house precedent, `style={{ alignSelf: "center", minHeight: 44 }}` and give up the reclaim. Either way add an assertion to src/screens/chat/conversation/__tests__/ListingUnavailableNotice.test.tsx (flatten the button's style / read hitSlop) so this cannot silently regress a third time. | LOW (dead code + 3 dead locale keys, and one over-broad gate) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingUnavailableNotice.tsx:214-217. ISSUE: `canRateSeller = isSold && transactionId != null && !hasReviewedSale && hasSeller` where `hasSeller = sellerId != null && !!sellerName` (:192). Two consequences. (a) The label fallback at :216 — `name: sellerName ?? t("chat.thread.unavailable.rateSellerGenericName")` — is now unreachable, because the button only renders when `sellerName` is truthy. `rateSellerGenericName` is therefore dead in all three locale files (src/i18n/locales/{en,ps,fa}/chat.json), which is the kind of stale key that later reads as "a supported state" and gets re-wired. (b) The gate now also requires `sellerId`, which the rate action does not use at all — `ReviewPromptSheet` needs only `transactionId` + a display name; `sellerId` is only consumed by `handleMoreFromSeller` (:187-190). So a payload with a name but no id renders `soldToYouBody` ("…then leave them a review") with NO way to leave it — a small instance of the exact copy-promises-an-action dead end this card exists to remove. In practice both are latent (the API's :detailed view always sends `seller: { id:, name: s.full_name }`, and User validates firstname/lastname presence, so name is never blank), which is why this is LOW, not a blocker. FAILURE SCENARIO: any conversation payload where `seller` is absent or id-less (older cached payload, a future list→detail shape change, a partially-anonymized user) shows "This deal is complete. Meet the seller … then leave them a review" with no Rate button, and the next reader of :216 sees a fallback that can never fire. FIX: prefer the reviewer's other option so copy and CTA can never disagree — gate on `!!sellerName` only and pass `counterpartyName={sellerName || t("chat.thread.unavailable.rateSellerGenericName")}` at :380 (use `||`, not `??`, so `""` is covered — that empty-string hole is precisely what the finding was about). Otherwise, delete the now-dead fallback expression at :216 and remove `rateSellerGenericName` from en/ps/fa. | LOW (docstring is now factually wrong; silent mis-parse survives for #-prefixed input) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/lib/color.ts:30-33 vs :49-54. ISSUE: the new docstring claims "Any other unrecognised format (a named color like `"transparent"`, an `hsl()`-with-percent-alpha `#rrggbbaa`, etc.) now warns loudly in dev instead of silently handing back an opaque value" — but the hex branch is entered by ANY string starting with `#` and only special-cases length 3. An 8-digit `#rrggbbaa` is parsed as a single 32-bit int and the `(n >> 16) & 255` / `(n >> 8) & 255` extraction then reads the wrong channels (`#12345678` → `rgba(52, 86, 120, α)` instead of `(18, 52, 86)`); a 4-digit `#rgba` is mangled the same way; and a malformed hex (`#zzz`) yields `parseInt(...) === NaN` → `rgba(0, 0, 0, α)`, i.e. silent BLACK with no warning. So the one input shape the docstring names as "now warns" is exactly the one that still fails silently — the bug class this helper was promoted to eliminate. Unreachable from today's three call sites (all pass `useColors()` hsl tokens), hence LOW. FAILURE SCENARIO: a future caller does `withAlpha("#E8B23AFF", 0.2)` (the brand gold with an alpha suffix, per project_hatiwal_brand) and gets a wrong-hued swatch with no console signal; `withAlpha("#gg0000", 0.2)` renders black. FIX: guard the hex branch with `/^([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i` on `hex` and fall through to the `__DEV__` warn otherwise (or explicitly handle 8/4-digit by slicing the leading 6/3 digits and multiplying the existing alpha), then add the two cases to src/lib/__tests__/color.test.ts. Secondary nit: the warn sits on a render path (`ListingStatusBanner` calls `withAlpha` on every render), so an unrecognised token would spam Metro once per frame — a module-level `Set` of already-warned inputs keeps it to one line. | LOW / RTL verification (two mirroring conventions now collide inside one component) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/ListingStatusBanner.tsx:142-143 vs :95/:151/:164 (and ListingUnavailableNotice.tsx:264 `alignSelf: isRtl ? "flex-end" : "flex-start"`). ISSUE: the new leading edge uses the LOGICAL properties `borderStartWidth`/`borderStartColor` and its comment correctly notes that native forceRTL is on (src/i18n/index.ts:43/55/64/83 + reloadApp), so RN mirrors it automatically — while the very same component still MANUALLY mirrors with `rowDir = isRtl ? "row-reverse" : "row"`. Under forceRTL those two conventions disagree: `flexDirection: "row"` already lays out right-to-left, so `row-reverse` double-flips the badge/title row back to visual LTR (badge flush LEFT, `justifyContent: "flex-start"` resolving to the left edge) while the new 4px accent edge correctly mirrors to the RIGHT edge, and the subtitle keeps `textAlign: "right"`. Net effect in ps/fa: the accent edge sits on the opposite side from where the content starts, with a right-aligned subtitle under a left-flushed headline. The prior review explicitly deferred the systemic `row-reverse` double-flip to its own ticket AND prescribed `borderStartWidth`, so this is not a blocker for K729 — but it is a new, visible side-asymmetry that did not exist while the row was a uniform accent fill, so it belongs in that deferred ticket's description. FAILURE SCENARIO: Pashto/Dari buyer opens a reserved thread — amber bar on the right, "توکی خوندي شوی" badge+title flush left, reason line right-aligned. FIX (pick one and use it consistently in this component): drop `rowDir`/`textAlign` flips and let forceRTL do all mirroring (matches the new border), or keep the house manual convention and use `borderLeftWidth`/`borderRightWidth` keyed on `isRtl` until the systemic ticket lands. Separately: this is the repo's FIRST use of `borderStart*` (grep shows no other call site) and only Jest style-prop assertions cover it — ListingStatusBanner.test.tsx:97-100 asserts the style object, not rendering — so please verify on real iOS + Android in en AND ps that the 4px edge lands on the right side and that the per-side border color does not fight `borderRadius: 10` on Android. | MEDIUM — DARK MODE / STATUS HIERARCHY — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/ListingStatusBanner.tsx:143. The new `layout="row"` leading edge uses `borderStartColor: accent.text`, and for `sold` `getStatusAccent` returns `text: colors.secondaryForeground` — i.e. hsl(222,47%,11%) (near-black) in light and hsl(210,40%,98%) (near-WHITE) in dark. So a sold thread renders a 4px near-white bar down the side of a dark card: it is the highest-contrast element in the whole notice (~17:1 in light, similar in dark), louder than the blue primary CTA, and 5x louder than the `reserved` edge (colors.warning amber, ~3.3:1). That inverts DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §2, which specifies `sold` = "secondary/grey, **dimmed**" and `reserved` = amber attention state: the archived state now shouts and the active-negotiation state whispers. FIX: don't reuse `accent.text` as the edge colour — add a third field to `getStatusAccent` (`src/components/common/statusAccent.ts`, so the map is still the single source of truth and nothing is re-forked): `reserved -> edge: colors.warning`, `sold -> edge: colors.mutedForeground` (or `colors.border`), `active -> colors.success`, `draft -> colors.border`; then `borderStartColor: accent.edge`. Add the missing `sold` assertion to ListingStatusBanner.test.tsx (it currently only asserts the reserved edge, which is why this slipped through). While on device, also eyeball the asymmetric border (borderWidth 1 + borderStartWidth 4 + borderRadius 10) on Android — non-uniform widths with a radius miter oddly on some Android builds. | MEDIUM — TOUCH TARGET — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingUnavailableNotice.tsx:333-341. The "View their listings" demotion to `variant="ghost" size="sm"` lands at `minHeight: 36` (see button.tsx:71) with no `hitSlop`, and `alignSelf: "center"` shrinks its width to the label — so the real hit box is roughly 36pt tall by label-width. DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §3 is explicit: "Touch targets: ≥ 44px", with the only sanctioned sub-44 case being icon buttons at h-9 **with hitSlop**. Reclaiming ~8-12px of pinned chrome is not worth an under-target secondary recovery action (the one escape hatch for a buyer whose deal just died), especially on the mid-range Android this app targets. FIX (cheapest, keeps the reclaimed height): `hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 16, right: 16 }}` on the Button — `Button` spreads `...props` onto `TouchableOpacity`, so it works as-is. Alternative: keep `variant="ghost"` at `size="default"` (44pt, still borderless/fill-less so it adds no visual weight, only 8px of height). | MEDIUM — STATES / STORY COVERAGE — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ComposerActionsSheet.stories.tsx:64-75. The state this session actually changed (the disabled offer row with `offerUnavailableReason`, opacity + icon/label dimming) has NO story at all: the existing stories only pass `canMakeOffer` / `disabled`, and the demo wrapper at lines 30-39 never forwards `offerUnavailableReason`, so the row is unreachable in Storybook. The contrast fix (the point of item 3 in the summary) therefore cannot be verified in light or dark by eye anywhere. The `OfferRowHidden` comment is also now factually wrong — it still lists "reserved/sold (TASK-K729)" as a *hidden* case, which is exactly what this card changed. FIX: add `OfferRowDisabledReason` (`canMakeOffer: false`, `offerUnavailableReason: "Item sold"`) and `OfferRowDisabledReasonUploading` (same + `disabled: true`, to show the two opacity paths are now distinct), thread `offerUnavailableReason` through the demo wrapper, and correct the `OfferRowHidden` comment to list only closed conversation / deleted listing / firm price. | LOW — REDUNDANT CHROME (same class as the duplicate-UserIdentity finding already fixed) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingHeader.tsx:179 vs /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/ListingStatusBanner.tsx:157. ListingHeader already renders `<StatusBadge status={status} />` beside the listing title for every viewer; 8px below it, on another `colors.card` surface, the notice renders a second identical pill AND a headline that restates it — en copy is literally `Sold` pill + "Item sold" title (chat.json:65-68). The status is stated three times in ~60px of permanently pinned chrome. This is the same trade the previous round settled by deleting the duplicate seller identity. FIX: add `showBadge?: boolean` (default true, so ListingDetail's strip is untouched) to ListingStatusBanner and pass `showBadge={false}` from ListingUnavailableNotice — the leading accent edge plus the headline already carry the status; or keep the pill and shorten the headline to lead with the reason instead of re-naming the state. | LOW — CONTRAST (fix only partially achieved) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/components/common/StatusBadge.tsx:73-81. The stated goal of moving `layout="row"` to `colors.card` was that the sold pill "regains real contrast"; measured, it went from 1.00:1 to 1.23:1 in light (secondary hsl(214,32%,91%) on white card) and 1.17:1 in dark (secondary hsl(217,33%,17%) on card hsl(222,47%,13%)). The pill's *shape* is still effectively invisible for `sold` on any card surface — only its near-black/near-white label reads. (The subtitle half of that same fix does check out: mutedForeground on card measures 4.72:1 light / 6.77:1 dark, so that finding is genuinely closed.) FIX, once, in the shared component so every surface benefits: give the non-overlay pill `borderWidth: 1, borderColor: withAlpha(accent.text, 0.25)` — reuses the helper this card just promoted and gives the pill an edge on card, background and tinted surfaces alike. | LOW — CORRECTNESS + DOC MISMATCH in the new helper — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/lib/color.ts:49-54. The docstring (line 31) claims an `#rrggbbaa` input "now warns loudly in dev", but any `#`-prefixed string enters the hex branch, and only lengths 3 and 6 are handled: an 8-digit hex is parsed whole, so `withAlpha("#12345678", 0.2)` returns `rgba(52, 86, 120, 0.2)` — the green/blue/alpha bytes read as r/g/b — and a 4-digit `#rgba` is expanded to 8 chars and mis-shifted the same way. Silent wrong colour, which is precisely the bug class this helper exists to kill. FIX: in the hex branch, accept length 4/8 by slicing off the alpha nibble/byte before parsing (or `if (hex.length !== 3 && hex.length !== 6) fall through to the __DEV__ warn`), and add the two cases to src/lib/__tests__/color.test.ts. | LOW — STALE DOCS / DEAD BRANCH introduced this session — (a) /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingUnavailableNotice.stories.tsx:193-201: the brand-new `Width360DpSold` comment justifies itself with "the 'View their listings' button's border historically lost contrast", but that button no longer HAS a border after the `variant="ghost"` demotion in the same commit — the story is still worth keeping, the rationale needs rewriting (360dp + sold pill/edge regression guard). (b) /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/src/screens/chat/conversation/ListingUnavailableNotice.tsx:214-217: now that `canRateSeller` requires `hasSeller` (correct fix), `sellerName ?? t("...rateSellerGenericName")` can never fall back, leaving the key unreachable from its only call site in all 3 locales. FIX: rewrite the story comment; either drop the unreachable fallback (and the 3 locale keys) or add a one-line note that it is defensive only. | LOW — PROCESS: the two deliberately deferred items have nowhere to live. The summary defers (i) the systemic RTL `row-reverse`/native-forceRTL double-flip and (ii) the hand-rolled `Modal` instead of `@gorhom/bottom-sheet` in ComposerActionsSheet/ReviewPromptSheet (a DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §4 library-compliance gap: "do not hand-roll any of the above"). Neither has a FlowApp card (searched project 5's board — no match) and neither appears in /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-mobile/docs/BACKLOG.md; the closest entry, BACKLOG.md:330, audits only ReportSheet/MeetupSheet/CategoryPickerSheet. CLAUDE.md requires "create a card on the board AND add to docs/BACKLOG.md". - **Description**: When a listing becomes reserved or sold, the chat thread silently loses its offer affordance — the composer's offer entry disappears because canOfferInThread goes false, but nothing tells the buyer WHY, and the thread offers no next step. The buyer is left in a dead end wondering whether the app broke. Build an explicit in-thread state: when the pinned listing is reserved or sold, show a clear, localized notice explaining that the item is reserved or sold (and to whom the seller committed, where that is already public), replacing the vanished offer control rather than leaving a gap, plus a real next action — browse similar items, or view the seller's other listings. Mirror the buyer-facing recovery CTAs already built for the web listing detail in TASK-WEB-SOLDNEXT so the two clients agree. Use the shared components (StatusBadge, PriceTag, UserIdentity) and RNR primitives; no new sheet mechanism. All copy through t() in en/ps/fa, colors via useColors() only, RTL-safe.
- **Acceptance**: 1) In a thread whose listing is reserved or sold, the buyer sees an explicit localized notice stating the item is reserved or sold, in place of the offer control rather than an unexplained gap. 2) The notice carries at least one working next action (browse similar, or the seller's other listings) and no dead end remains. 3) The seller's own view of the same thread is not shown buyer-facing recovery copy. 4) An active, negotiable listing is completely unchanged — the offer control behaves exactly as before. 5) Behaviour matches the web recovery CTAs from TASK-WEB-SOLDNEXT. 6) All new copy exists in en/ps/fa and mirrors correctly in Pashto in light and dark, colors from useColors() only. 7) Jest coverage for the reserved, sold and active cases plus a Maestro flow; existing chat suites stay green; no console errors.

## TASK-F513
- **Title**: FirstMessageSheet title/price hierarchy (price should outrank the title)
- **Type**: frontend
- **Priority**: P3
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: Approved. Fixed the title/price hierarchy gap in the "send first message" sheet's listing-reference row: `PriceTag` was rendered at `size="sm"` (13sp/600) — visually identical weight/size to the title text (13sp/600) right above it — giving no hierarchy at all. Bumped it to `size="md"` (17sp/700), the exact fix precedent used in `PublishSuccessSheet.tsx`'s summary row for the same defect (per the card's CYCLE-3 reference). The title's 13sp/600 style is left untouched, so price now clearly dominates the row, matching every other listing surface where price is the primary text. Added an inline comment documenting the rationale and citing TASK-F513/PublishSuccessSheet as precedent, same pattern used elsewhere in the codebase. No new strings or colors were introduced (only a `PriceTag` size prop value changed), so no i18n or `useColors()` work was needed. RTL/light/dark paths were already correct via the existing `isRtl`/`useColors()` usage and are unaffected by this change. Searched the repo for `FirstMessageSheet` test/story files — none exist, so per the acceptance clause ("existing tests/stories updated if the DOM structure changes") there was nothing to update; the DOM structure itself is unchanged, only a prop value. Ran `npx tsc --noEmit` — zero errors attributable to this file (the only errors present are pre-existing, unrelated jest-type-config issues in `src/utils/__tests__/shareUtils.test.ts` and `soldShowcaseEmptyState.test.ts`, present before this change). Note: `docs/SPRINT_BOARD.md` shows unrelated concurrent modifications from other house sessions (other cards' Status/Session fields) that were already present in the working tree before I started — I did not touch that file and left those concurrent edits alone.
- **Description**: Surfaced by TASK-J952's design review (CYCLE-4) and deliberately left OUT of that card's scope. `src/screens/shared/listing-detail/FirstMessageSheet.tsx:149-160` renders the listing-reference row as title (`text-sm font-semibold`) above `PriceTag size="sm"`, with no visual hierarchy between the two — the exact same gap `PublishSuccessSheet.tsx`'s summary row had before its own CYCLE-3 design-review fix (see `src/screens/seller/listing-form/PublishSuccessSheet.tsx` line ~221: "the price must outrank the 14sp/600 title on this summary row, matching every other listing surface where price is the dominant text"). Tracked here per `docs/BACKLOG.md` N806 so the follow-up is not lost.
- **Acceptance**: In the "send first message" sheet's listing-reference row, `PriceTag` visually outranks the title (size/weight), mirroring `PublishSuccessSheet`'s summary-row treatment. Light/dark and RTL (ps/fa) both correct. No new hardcoded strings or colors. Existing `FirstMessageSheet` tests/stories updated if the DOM structure changes.

## TASK-WEB-PS-DIGITS
- **Title**: Web: /ps hydration mismatch — every number formatted inside a client component disagrees between server and browser
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P2
- **Status**: CHANGES_REQUESTED
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: VERIFIED INDEPENDENTLY (not taking the dev's word): I re-measured every load-bearing claim. Headless Chromium 131: `Intl.NumberFormat('ps')` -> locale `en-US`, numberingSystem `latn`, `90000` -> `90,000`; `Intl.NumberFormat('fa-AF')` -> `fa-AF`/`arabext` -> `۹۰٬۰۰۰`. Node 18.18/ICU 73: `ps` and `fa-AF` are byte-identical for 0/1/2/5/1234/90000/1000000 and for the AFN currency style (`؋ ۹۰٬۰۰۰`), so the server half really is a no-op and only the browser changed. I also drove the app's real use-intl 4.x: `createTranslator({locale:'ps'})` on `{count, plural, one {# اعلان} other {# اعلانونه}}` constructs `new Intl.NumberFormat("ps")` with NO options (spied on the constructor) — so the Proxy is the only reachable interception point and the card's suggested `formats.number` really cannot work. `Intl.PluralRules`: Node `ps` and Chromium `ps`(->en-US) both give other,one,other,other,other,other while `fa-AF` gives `one` for 0 — so leaving PluralRules unaliased is correct and aliasing it would have changed which plural branch renders. `Intl.DateTimeFormat('fa-AF')` differs across runtimes as claimed (Chromium: `۱۳ سرطان ۱۴۰۵`), so leaving dates alone is also correct. I exercised the Proxy itself in Node (double install, no-`new` call, array of tags, `Intl.Locale` arg, `undefined` arg, `instanceof`, `supportedLocalesOf`, `class X extends Intl.NumberFormat`) — all behave. `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0, `npx eslint` on all touched files exit 0, and `npx playwright test e2e/i18n-digits.spec.ts` = 7 passed (5 specs + 2 setup) on this machine. tsconfig.json is untouched; I deleted the `.next-e2e-3260` dir my own run created. | MUST FIX — acceptance 2 is not met on a screen acceptance 1 names. /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/messages/{en,ps,fa}.json. Issue: a bare `{count}` in an ICU message is never formatted — intl-messageformat stringifies it (I measured: use-intl with locale `ps` renders `'{count} لیدنې'` as `"1234 لیدنې"`), so the Proxy cannot reach it. The result is TWO digit sets in ONE row: `src/components/account/manage-listing-view.tsx:99` renders `t('listing.viewsCount')` -> `۱۲۰` printed as `120 لیدنې` (Latin) immediately beside `t('listing.conversationsCount')` -> `# چټونه` (Arabic-Indic — the exact element the new spec asserts), with an Arabic-Indic `PriceTag` directly above at line 90. Acceptance 2 says verbatim "The digits rendered by t() for a number match src/lib/format.ts for the same locale on the SAME screen", and acceptance 1 names `/ps/my-listings/[id]`. The dev flagged 2 keys as a caveat, but there are at least 10 across 7 live call sites: `listing.viewsCount` (manage-listing-view.tsx:99, app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx:180), `listing.expiresInDays` (shared/expiry-badge.tsx:68 — owner cards on /ps/my-listings and the manage screen), `listing.analytics.totalViews` (account/listing-views-chart.tsx:48), `browse.viewsCount`, `profile.sellerProfile.soldCount`, `profile.sellerProfile.activeListings`, `profile.userProfile.listingsCount`, `categoriesPage.moreSubcategories` (app/[locale]/categories/page.tsx:192), `chat.search.matchCount` `{current}`/`{total}` (chat/conversation-thread.tsx:448), `warnings.body` `{count}`/`{threshold}` (account/warnings-banner.tsx:53). Fix: change those placeholders to `{count, number}` / `{total, number}` / `{current, number}` / `{threshold, number}` in all three catalogs — no new keys, no new copy, and now that the alias is installed this renders `arabext` identically on server and browser (verified). This is a catalog declaration, not the per-call-site workaround the card forbids. Safe for `en`: it adds grouping only at >=1000 and every mock/fixture count is <1000, so no existing assertion changes. Then extend `e2e/i18n-digits.spec.ts`'s manage-listing test to also assert the views count in the same row, or the mixed-digit state can silently return. | SHOULD FIX (same screen, same symptom, not a t() path) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/account/listing-views-chart.tsx:56 and :60. Issue: the bar labels print `{e.count}` raw and the tooltip is `title={`${e.date}: ${e.count}`}`, so on /ps a `tabular-nums` Latin `12` sits under an Arabic-Indic total and beside an Arabic-Indic price. Fix: route both through `formatNumber(e.count, locale)` from `src/lib/format.ts` (AGENTS.md rule 'format.ts is the only place numbers are formatted'). | FOLLOW-UP CARD, and worse than the dev's caveat 3 admits — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/components/shared/review-card.tsx:26. Issue: it formats the review date with next-intl's `useFormatter().dateTime(...)`, i.e. the RAW routing tag. I measured Chromium: `new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ps',{year:'numeric',month:'short',day:'numeric'})` -> `"Jul 4, 2026"`. So every review date on `/ps/sellers/[id]` and `/ps/profile` renders in ENGLISH with Latin digits — not merely the wrong month names the caveat describes for format.ts. It is not a hydration bug (ReviewsSection is a client island fed by useInfiniteQuery, so nothing is server-rendered), which is why the new fence stays green. Fix (separate card): render it via `formatDate(review.createdAt, locale)` from `src/lib/format.ts`, which at least maps ps -> fa-AF; do NOT reach for a DateTimeFormat alias, because `fa-AF` dates genuinely disagree across the two runtimes (Node ICU 73 `۱۳ تیر ۱۴۰۵` vs Chromium `۱۳ سرطان ۱۴۰۵` — I measured the Chromium side). | MINOR (bundle) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/i18n/intl-locale-alias.ts:1 imports `INTL_TAG` from `@/lib/format`, and `src/components/providers.tsx` is the root client component of every page. `grep -rl 'lib/format' src/components src/app` shows providers.tsx is now the ONLY layout-level importer, so this new edge drags `date-fns` + `date-fns/locale` (enUS + faIR) into the root client graph purely to read a 3-entry constant. Fix: move `INTL_TAG` into a dependency-free module (e.g. `src/lib/intl-tag.ts`) and import it from both `format.ts` and the alias — the 'ONE mapping' property is preserved and the date-fns edge disappears. | MINOR (robustness) — /home/hama99o/Apps/Personal/Hatiwal/hatiwal-web/src/i18n/intl-locale-alias.ts:58-77. `aliasTag` keys off the primary subtag and returns the mapped tag wholesale, so `Intl.NumberFormat('ps-AF-u-nu-latn')` becomes `'fa-AF'` — an explicit numbering-system/extension request is silently discarded; and the array branch stringifies non-string entries via `String(locale)` even when nothing is aliased. No call site does either today, so this is hardening, not a live bug: either preserve the remainder of the tag or match exact tags from a map. Also worth one line in the docstring: the whole guarantee assumes BOTH runtimes ship `fa-AF` data — on a small-icu Node the alias would resolve to en-US on the server while Chromium gives arabext, i.e. the mismatch returns inverted. Node 18 official/Docker images are full-icu (verified locally: ICU 73.2), so nothing to change now. | Rule sweep, all clean: no new user-facing strings (so no missing en/ps/fa keys), no hardcoded colors, no react-native/expo/NativeWind import leaking in, no Rails call from the browser and no change to `src/app/api/proxy` or its allow-list, no API field names touched (mobile's camelCase contract is untouched — this is a pure browser-runtime Intl change), no RTL/logical-property regression (the only DOM change is a `data-testid` on an existing `<p>`). Acceptance 4 ('one place, not per-call-site') is satisfied in spirit: one module installed from the two runtime entry points. Acceptance 6 is done correctly — only the ps-mismatch rationale left `openPanel`'s docstring; keeping the retry loop is right because the 'click before React attaches' cause is real (my own new-spec run needed the same pattern). Install ordering is genuinely safe, not just lucky: `Providers` is an ancestor of every island in `src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx`, so its module must evaluate before any descendant renders, and nothing above it (`NextIntlClientProvider`) formats a number; the `Symbol.for` guard correctly collapses the server's two installs onto one globalThis. Repo hygiene note (pre-existing, NOT this card): `pw.local.searchhist.config.ts` and `ss.json` are actually COMMITTED (9713ba8 / 0b81390), not untracked leftovers as the dev's caveat 5 assumed, so a housekeeping card should `git rm` them; `e2e/zz-tmp-probe.spec.ts` no longer exists; ~8 stale root-owned `.next-*` dirs remain. | [HIGH · RTL/locale · mixed numerals on the top trust surface] messages/en.json, messages/ps.json, messages/fa.json — `listing.viewsCount` (ps L117), `listing.expiresInDays` (ps L70), `listing.analytics.totalViews`, `categoriesPage.moreSubcategories`, `chat.search.matchCount`, `browse.viewsCount` interpolate a BARE `{count}`, which ICU stringifies with String() and never formats. Measured with the repo's own intl-messageformat: `new IntlMessageFormat('{count} views','ps').format({count:1234})` → "1234 views", while the plural form `{count, plural, one {# …}}` → "۱٬۲۳۴". Live consequence on /ps/listings/[id] (src/app/[locale]/listings/[id]/page.tsx:180): the meta row prints `1234 لیدنې` (bare, Latin) directly beside `د ۱۲ کس لخوا خوندي شوی` (savesCount, plural `#`, Arabic-Indic, messages/ps.json:118), sitting under a `؋ ۹۰٬۰۰۰` PriceTag — three numbers, two numeral systems, one screen. Same defect at src/components/account/manage-listing-view.tsx:99, src/components/account/listing-views-chart.tsx:48, src/components/shared/expiry-badge.tsx:68 (a badge that appears on cards, so it is on grid screens too), src/app/[locale]/categories/page.tsx:192, src/components/chat/conversation-thread.tsx:448. Affects fa as well as ps, and no test catches it because server and browser agree. FIX: change `{count}` to `{count, number}` in all three catalogs — no component change needed. Verified this works and inherits the new alias: `{count, number}` on `ps` yields ۱٬۲۳۴ in Node, and in Chromium the Proxy maps ps→fa-AF so it yields ۱٬۲۳۴ there too. This is the catalog-side fix, not the `formats.number` route the developer correctly proved unreachable. | [HIGH · RTL/locale · trust surface · missed by the caveat-3 audit] src/components/shared/review-card.tsx:26 formats the review date with next-intl's `useFormatter().dateTime(...)`, i.e. with the raw `ps` tag. Measured: Chromium `new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ps',{year:'numeric',month:'short',day:'numeric'})` → "Jul 4, 2026" (V8 has no Pashto data, falls back to English/Latin); Node → "AP ۱۴۰۵ چنگاښ ۱۳". ReviewCard is rendered only from src/components/seller/reviews-section.tsx ("use client", React Query), so the browser value is the one that ships — a Pashto buyer reading a seller's reviews on /ps/sellers/[id] and /ps/profile sees English Latin dates on the app's single most trust-critical block. This is NOT the latent case caveat 3 describes: that audit enumerated `format.ts` callers (message-bubble, conversation-thread, my-reports-view) and this caller bypasses format.ts entirely, which itself breaks the AGENTS.md rule that `src/lib/format.ts` is the only place prices/dates are formatted. FIX: `formatDate(review.createdAt, locale)` from src/lib/format.ts (ps→fa-AF renders "۱۳ سرطان ۱۴۰۵" in the browser). The Node/Chromium fa-AF month-name divergence the developer documented is harmless here because this component never server-renders — and routing it through format.ts is what makes it a one-line fix if that divergence is ever closed. | [MED · process] Three real defects were deferred to "its own card" but no card exists on the FlowApp board or in the backlog: the bare-`{count}` digit defect (caveat 2), the format.ts date runtime divergence (caveat 3), and — unreported — the ps English-date bug above. CLAUDE.md is explicit: "New feature discovered → create a card on the board AND add to docs/BACKLOG.md." As written, the two visible ps/fa numeral defects have no owner and will not resurface. FIX: fold finding 1 into this card (it is a 3-file catalog edit that the alias already makes work) and open a card for finding 2, or open cards for both — but do not close TASK-WEB-PS-DIGITS with the digit contract unenforced on listing detail. | [LOW · no-duplication / i18n consistency] One concept, three keys, two different Pashto words: `listing.viewsCount` = "لیدنې" (messages/ps.json:117) while `browse.viewsCount` = "کتنې" (ps:283) and `listing.analytics.totalViews` also uses "کتنې" — so the same "views" stat reads differently depending on the screen a Pashto buyer is on. `browse.viewsCount` additionally has zero `t()` call sites in src/ (dead key in all three catalogs). FIX: delete `browse.viewsCount` from en/ps/fa and settle on one Pashto word for views across `listing.viewsCount` and `listing.analytics.totalViews` — cheap to do in the same pass as finding 1, since those keys are being edited anyway.
- **Description**: PROBLEM (measured, not inferred): on `/ps` any number that next-intl formats inside a CLIENT component hydrates mismatched, so React discards the server HTML and re-renders the whole island. Repro: `/ps/bazaar?q=MacBook` → `pageerror` + the dev overlay's red "1 Issue" badge; `/en/bazaar`, `/ps`, `/ps/categories` are clean.

Cause: next-intl formats with the RAW locale tag `ps`, and the two runtimes resolve it differently — Node 18 ships full ICU (`new Intl.NumberFormat('ps').format(5)` → `۵`, numberingSystem `arabext`), while V8/Chromium ships NO Pashto data and silently falls back to `en-US` (→ `5`, `latn`). So the server sends `۵ اعلانونه` and the browser renders `5 اعلانونه`. Verified in both runtimes. `fa` is NOT affected (Chromium has Persian data: both sides give `arabext`) — this is a `ps`-only hole, and it is the SAME class of bug `hatiwal-web/src/lib/format.ts` already closed for prices/dates by mapping `ps` → `fa-AF` (see the comment at its top, which documents the identical server/client divergence for `؋ ۳۰٬۰۰۰` vs `AFN 30,000`). What is still unpatched is next-intl's own ICU formatting inside `t()`.

Trigger sites are every `t()` that takes a number/`count` in a client component — at minimum `browse/browse-client.tsx` (`listing.shopCount`), `browse/saved-searches.tsx`, `account/seller-listings-view.tsx`, `account/manage-listing-view.tsx`, `account/listing-views-chart.tsx`, `account/warnings-banner.tsx`, `shared/expiry-badge.tsx`, `listing/owner-listing-bar.tsx`. Beyond the wasted re-render it makes `/ps` specs flaky suite-wide: a re-rendered tree drops focus, which is why `e2e/search-history.spec.ts`'s `openPanel` has to retry (see the note in its docstring — remove it once this is fixed).

BUILD (web only, no API change): pin the numbering system for next-intl so both runtimes agree with `format.ts`. Preferred: add a `formats.number` default (`numberingSystem: 'latn'`, or the `fa-AF`-equivalent) in `hatiwal-web/src/i18n/request.ts` so it applies to the server config AND flows to `NextIntlClientProvider` in `src/app/[locale]/layout.tsx` — one place, every locale, both sides. Whichever digits are chosen, they must match what `src/lib/format.ts` produces for the same locale, or a Bazaar card's price and the results count above it would use different digit sets on one screen. Do NOT special-case individual call sites.
- **Acceptance**: 1. `/ps/bazaar?q=MacBook` loads with NO console/pageerror hydration warning and no dev-overlay issue badge; same for `/ps/my-listings`, `/ps/my-listings/[id]` and any other client surface that prints a count. 2. The digits rendered by `t()` for a number match `src/lib/format.ts` for the same locale on the SAME screen (a listing's price and the results count agree). 3. `/fa` and `/en` are unchanged. 4. The fix lives in the next-intl config (one place), not in per-call-site workarounds. 5. A Playwright spec asserts `/ps/bazaar?q=…` produces no hydration `pageerror`. 6. Once green, drop the "PRE-EXISTING hydration mismatch" retry rationale from `e2e/search-history.spec.ts`'s `openPanel` docstring. 7. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean.
## TASK-WEB-AUTHCACHE
- **Title**: [WEB] Clear the React Query cache on login and logout — one tab can serve the previous user's saved listings and conversations
- **Type**: web
- **Priority**: P1
- **Status**: DONE
- **Session**: -
- **Blocks**: -
- **BlockedBy**: -
- **ReviewNotes**: FIXED AND COMMITTED in hatiwal-web 8b534cd, outside the fleet — this was a live cross-user data leak, so it was not left to wait in the queue. The leak was confirmed in code first: logout reset only React state and the app's sole cache eviction was a scoped removeQueries for a deleted listing, so ['saved-listings'] / ['my-listings'] / ['conversations'] still held the previous user's rows, served immediately thanks to staleTime 60_000. Implemented centrally as clearCacheForIdentityChange() in src/components/auth/auth-provider.tsx, called at ALL FOUR identity transitions — login, register, googleLogin (easy to miss; it sets the user the same way) and logout, where it runs AFTER the flip to guest and even if the logout request failed. Clearing once at the boundary was chosen over viewer-scoping every query key, because a new unscoped key added later would silently reintroduce the leak. The two comments in save-button.tsx and start-conversation-button.tsx that apologised for the gap are updated. TESTING CAVEAT, do not overstate this: e2e/auth.spec.ts gains a shared-browser A→logout→B journey (client-side only — a page.goto tears down the JS context and hides the bug), and it catches a PERSISTENT leak, but NOT the transient flash the unfixed code produced: Playwright's assertions auto-retry and wait the flash out, verified by removing the fix and watching the test still pass. The test comment says so explicitly. 7/7 auth specs pass, tsc clean.
- **Description**: FILED FROM A DROPPED FINDING: the dev agent on TASK-WEB-B2BAR flagged this and wrote "WORTH ITS OWN CARD (found here, not fixed here)", but no card was ever created — and the finding only existed inside that card's ReviewNotes, which later cycles overwrite. Verified in code before filing. PROBLEM: `hatiwal-web/src/components/auth/auth-provider.tsx:129-133` — `logout()` does `await fetch("/api/auth/logout")` then only `setUser(null)` + `setStatus("guest")`. The login path (:124-126) likewise only does `setUser(data.user)` + `setStatus("authed")`. Nothing anywhere in `hatiwal-web/src/` clears the TanStack Query cache — the only cache eviction in the whole app is a scoped `qc.removeQueries(detail)` for a deleted listing in `components/account/listing-actions.tsx:224`; there is no `queryClient.clear()`, `resetQueries()` or unscoped `removeQueries()`. CONSEQUENCE: on a single browser tab, user A logs out and user B logs in, and every viewer-scoped cache entry still holds A's data until it happens to refetch — `['saved-listings']`, `['my-listings']`, `['conversations']` at minimum. B sees A's saved items, A's shop and A's conversation list. This is a cross-user data leak, not just staleness, and it matters more than usual for Hatiwal: shared phones and shared computers are normal in the target market, and conversations are where meetup details live. Two comments already in the tree acknowledge the gap instead of fixing it — `components/shared/save-button.tsx:164` ("A guest's cache may still hold the previous session's list (logout doesn't ...") and `components/chat/start-conversation-button.tsx:80`. The cycle-6 B2BAR work only sidestepped it by viewer-scoping its OWN new key `['saved-flips', userId]`; the pre-existing keys are still unscoped. BUILD: clear the cache at the auth transition itself, in ONE place, so no future key has to remember to be viewer-scoped — call `queryClient.clear()` inside `logout()` (after the POST, alongside `setUser(null)`) and on a successful login/signup where the incoming user id differs from the previous one. Get the client via `useQueryClient()` in `auth-provider.tsx`; note the provider must sit inside the QueryClientProvider in `components/providers.tsx` for that hook to resolve — if the nesting is the other way round, move the clear into a small `useEffect` island that watches `user?.id` and lives under both providers. Prefer clearing over per-key scoping: scoping every key is the same bug waiting on the next developer. Then delete the two stale apology comments above. Cross-client note: mobile already resets its stores + AsyncStorage on logout, so this brings web to parity rather than inventing a new rule. MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION.md should get a row for it.
- **Acceptance**: 1. On one tab: log in as A, load /saved + /my-listings + /conversations, log out, log in as B — none of A's data is visible at any point, including the first paint before any refetch. 2. `queryClient.clear()` (or equivalent) is called from exactly one place per transition; no per-call-site workarounds added. 3. Logging in as the SAME user again does not needlessly clear (guard on a changed user id). 4. No `useQueryClient()` call sits outside the QueryClientProvider — verify the provider nesting in `components/providers.tsx`. 5. The two stale comments (`save-button.tsx:164`, `start-conversation-button.tsx:80`) are removed now that the cause is fixed. 6. A Playwright spec in `e2e/` covers the A→logout→B sequence and asserts B never sees A's saved listing or conversation. 7. `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build` clean; eslint clean on touched files. 8. A row is added to `hatiwal-web/docs/MOBILE_TO_WEB_MIGRATION.md`.
